  instead require the exact incomplete cause and transactional cleanup and
  contribute no performance-acceptance numerator; any mismatch is still a red
  release-gate failure. An omitted workload or
  identity mismatch is not evaluated and cannot make the ledger green;

- parse, bind, lower, seal, Program-artifact encode/validate/decode, engine
  compile, solve, and query time reported separately;
- p50, p95, maximum, total wall time, allocations, and peak RSS;
- cold and warm solve;
- source-built versus stored-artifact module load, link, and first Query;
- one-line edit in one module: shard relink, invalidated equations/transfers,
  allocations, and retained untouched-shard cache work;
- one, ten, and one hundred equal-projection callers;
- distinct projections that differ in one value, heap key, callback, type row,
  and effect row;
- deep-library placement/effect proof substitution;
- evidence construction and checking: node/premise-edge counts, hash-cons
  hits/misses, retained bytes, validation time, and on-demand rendering time
  for shared diamonds, high fan-in/retraction, deep boundaries, and forced
  cancellation. Epoch-live bytes and retained process interner/cache bytes are
  reported separately; forced cancellation returns epoch-live evidence bytes
  to zero. Repeated identical and distinct forced-cancel workloads may not
  publish certificate-eligible retained evidence. Their retained entries are
  absent until completion or evictable under the declared performance-cache
  policy, and the ledger records a finite retained-byte plateau/limit rather
  than allowing canceled epochs to grow process memory without bound;
- actor-local/shared placement and preallocation fact workloads, including
  nested transfer, mutation-before/after-send, retention, and suspension;
- branch-partition and recursive-context stress;
- localized head publication reports `|M_K|`, selected target-key count,
  unselected exact-R updates, touched Factor operations, and the separately
  unavoidable flat-vector/exact-R `O(F)` work. It performs no all-F recurrence-
  membership discovery and no all-F Widen/Narrow dispatch; ordinary exact-R
  construction may visit/copy the separately reported Factor vector;
- producer-fan-in/retraction with increasing `deg(p)` and both sparse and dense
  declared writes/Carries: repeatedly replace and retract one producer and
  report exact-refold or reduction-tree time, allocations, touched planes, BDD
  nodes, peak live bytes, and Point-consumer wake counts. The gate must expose
  hidden `O(deg(p)*F)` storage/work and is the only evidence that may authorize
  a high-fan-in reduction-tree representation over the required baseline exact
  refold;
- one exact demanded Factor/key with `F = {1,16,100}` total Factors, adding
  only disconnected Factors and Rules: identical State and Query result, and
  invariant semantic Point/Group count, Product units/callback rows, active SCC
  membership, cyclic members, and rank obligations. This is a semantic-work
  law, not a claim that total hot solve wall time is flat: every required flat
  outer-vector construction, attachment, Join, Widen, or Narrow copy is
  separately counted as its unavoidable `O(F)` physical work, including copied
  slots, wall time, allocations, and bytes;
- one demanded cyclic Factor with a valid rank plus an unranked disconnected
  sibling: seal and solve exactly as without the sibling. The obsolete
  expectation that an unrelated unranked sibling blocks sealing is reversed
  and retired in favor of this rooted Point-component law;
- second Query performs no solving.

Carrier microbenchmarks are a separate release gate. They cover
`P = {3,9,16,25}` sealed Factors; Product arity `N = {1,2,4}`; read widths of
zero, one exact key, four exact keys across distinct planes, and one explicit
whole-plane dynamic read; populated keys per plane `{1,16,128}`; and shared
versus independent Guard decisions. They report operation count, callback-row
count, wall time, allocations, bytes, and peak live bytes for Restrict,
Product, Equal, LessOrEq, Join, Widen, Narrow, `Mu`, delta publication, and a
complete recurrent solve. The invariant gate is stronger than a timing
envelope: with fixed guards, inputs, and one exact-key read, Product partition,
fingerprint/equality, and callback work are independent of unrelated `P` and
unrelated keys. A no-read Rule has exactly one support row, and an opaque Carry
adds zero partitions. Only the explicit whole-plane read may scale with that
plane's populated contents. A failure is an implementation defect, never a
reason to collapse semantics or raise a budget.

The disconnected-factor matrix separately fits the unavoidable flat-vector
construction/copy cost as `O(F)`. Its checked-in pinned-machine baseline names
literal acceptable intercept and per-slot constants for vector construction
time, copy time, allocations, and bytes; a run fails when any measured linear
coefficient exceeds its named constant. Those constants are physical release
criteria at the stated measurement widths, not Factor/admission/input capacity
budgets, and do not make total hot solve work flat or permit a precision loss.

The frozen target-language contract also owns per-family scale workloads,
including adversarial Guard/product correlation, dynamic application/SCC
growth, heap-key partitions, recursive effects, and publication/lifecycle
cycles. Each reports input scale, completed/incomplete status, wall time,
allocations, and peak RSS. Performance envelopes are release acceptance
criteria, not semantic cutoffs: exceeding one is a visible performance failure
requiring optimization or more resources, never permission to collapse a
Factor or publish a partial answer.

No performance gate may hide missing semantic work. A regression is accepted
only with a measured semantic gain and explicit approval.

## Scope and necessity discipline

Every proposed mechanism must name the invariant it proves and the existing
consumer that requires it. If it proves no semantic soundness, termination,
publication, persistence, diagnostic, or measured complexity invariant, it is
not implemented. “Useful later”, symmetry with another subsystem, easier
instrumentation, and defensive generality are not requirements.

Ownership is deliberately narrow:

- Target owns frozen dialect/provider operation and lifecycle laws,
  initial-environment rows, and policy constants;
- Program owns source language structure, source identity, and canonical core
  persistence identity;
- Link owns project binding, provider/module instances, exact per-boundary
  correspondence and Guard transport, and resolved-link identity;
- the private equation compiler owns only structural nodes/edges, SCC/WTO and
  compiled `Mu` identity, plus deterministic evaluation order;
- the private guarded Factor carrier owns current alternatives and values;
- dependency storage owns current semantic reads and invalidation;
- State owns only immutable Query projections and provenance;
- domains own abstractions, judgments, widening/narrowing, cleanup, evidence,
  and precision loss;
- backend/runtime owns representation choice, allocation/COW policy, GC,
  safepoints, execution scheduling, and machine resource policy.

From that ownership follow mandatory simplifications:

- monotone structural caches are not made solve-transactional; only one local
  structural delta must install atomically;
- cancellation discards semantic work and publication, not validated topology;
- no snapshot, generation, handle, registry, projection, transport, or
  lifecycle type is introduced when an existing owner and identity already
  state the invariant;
- no engine feature predicts backend/runtime policy; it publishes only the
  typed facts that a later consumer actually needs;
- no future integration receives an API before a current semantic law consumes
  it;
- no optimization adds a second semantic route; misses take the same canonical
  route and caches change work only;
- an implementation noun stays private and is removed when its distinct
  invariant disappears.

Before implementation, each feature is classified as one of: semantic law,
termination mechanism, persistence boundary, observable Query requirement, or
measured performance mechanism. Unclassified features are deleted from the
plan. Tests prove the owned semantic or mathematical law, never the existence
of the mechanism itself.

## Semantic gates

No test is named or described as the full oracle unless it enumerates the
complete checked-in expectation ledger and every root in its declared source
denominator. A narrow fixture test is deleted rather than retained as a
convenient green signal.

The canonical oracle ledger is a checked-in, versioned data artifact, not Go
test discovery. One record contains the canonical denominator tuple: fixture
identity; ordered fixture-source inventory and digest; ordered expectation-ID
inventory and canonical expectation-payload schema/version and digest; sealed
Target-contract, host-contract, Query/diagnostic-schema, and root-set-schema
IDs; the frozen host/deployment/Link-input digest; the resolved-module and
sealed Link-relation digests; the ordered actor-boot and actor-local-root
mapping digest; the ordered root-to-`ModuleCacheInstance` representative
ingress/transport digest; and the complete ordered root set and its canonical
content digest. It also contains a total map from every stable expectation ID
to its own disposition:
`Analyze`,
`ExpectedUnsupported(diagnostic)`, `Reexpress(query expectation)`, or
`Retired(reason, replacement law)`. There is no fixture-wide disposition: one
obsolete placement assertion cannot retire an unrelated diagnostic or source
root in the same fixture. The expectation manifest is the sole authority for
its payload; the ledger stores only its canonical schema/content digest and
dispositions, never a copied expected value or diagnostic payload.

A repository enumerator independently derives that complete tuple, without
consulting the ledger: every fixture source and expectation ID/payload from the
frozen fixture inventory; and every required analysis root from the frozen
Target plus host/deployment/Link inputs, including resolved modules, host
exposures, actor boots, actor-local root mapping, and every root-to-
`ModuleCacheInstance` representative ingress/transport row. It canonically
seals those Link inputs, derives the resolved relation and its digests, orders
the roots under the sealed root-set schema, and independently recomputes every
tuple digest. The inventory gate fails unless every tuple coordinate and digest
equals the ledger exactly. A ledger cannot add, omit, or reclassify a root; an
unbound, duplicate, extra, cross-actor-shared-cache, or untransported derived
root fails before execution. Reusing a stable expectation ID with changed
payload therefore fails before execution. The source denominator is every
`(fixture, source content ID)` unconditionally. The execution denominator is
every independently derived root; an expected parse/bind/lower/Link rejection
closes only with its exact structural diagnostic and otherwise every root must
complete Solve. The expectation denominator is every mapped expectation ID,
reported separately by disposition. A retired expectation closes only when its
reason and replacement law resolve and the obsolete check is not executed; it
never appears as an analyzed or passing expectation. Reports show inventory,
source, execution, analyzed, unsupported, re-expressed, and retired counts
separately. Source, Target, host, deployment/Link input, Query/diagnostic
schema, root, or expectation changes require the matching frozen declaration
and an explicit ledger change, including a payload edit under an unchanged
stable expectation ID.
Legacy native and placement expectations must be re-expressed as canonical
Queries or retired with a named replacement law; they are never silently
copied, omitted, or treated as analyzer facts.

The current analyzer report gives separate exact counts for:

- parser inputs and successful bind/Program lower/seal;
- declared solve roots, completed roots, and incomplete roots;
- expected, emitted, missing, and unexpected diagnostics;
- severity, trust, span, evidence, and native/provider-fact mismatches;
- expectation-payload schema/content-digest mismatches;
- Program-artifact encode/validate/decode equivalence;
- convergence and performance failures.

Compiler, bytecode, VM/runtime, V2, backend, and JIT work are absent from this
report and cannot be counted as analyzer progress. Only completed roots
contribute semantic numerators. Cancellation, timeout, allocation failure, or
any other resource failure leaves the root in the denominator, marks the gate
red, and publishes no State, Query, diagnostic, evidence, certificate, or
semantic cache result.

Before a Factor or Rule enters the complete composition, its child package
passes executable laws for bottom/top, order, join, default/presence,
soundness against its declared concretization, monotonicity, boundary
substitution, pointwise widening extrapolation `a ⊔ b ⊑ a ▽ b`, widening
idempotence `a ▽ a = a`, strict descent of a well-founded rank for every
changed proposal `a ▽ b`, stabilization under adversarial arbitrary proposal
sequences, plus narrowing sandwich/rank descent and narrowing/revocation only
for Factors in the tested cyclic Point component's derived recurrence closure.
Tests exercise every sealed transfer shape and a
history-sensitive cyclic composition; they never obtain convergence by a case,
depth, node, context, or iteration budget.
These suites are regression evidence. Admission separately records whether the
universal algebra/transfer obligation was discharged exhaustively, by a checked
proof artifact, or by the explicitly versioned trusted theorem/checker required
above; a sampled suite alone cannot claim that status.

Carrier laws separately prove support-only Restrict observational equivalence,
the complete outer-support equality/order laws, per-plane under-support
Equal/LessOrEq equivalence to the mathematical joint product, three-region
Join/Widen/Narrow/`Mu` (including disjoint support and rejection of invalid
root reuse), exact added/removed-support and update-produced plane deltas
against exhaustive comparison, and structural acceptance batching over
separately identified Factor-output Rule patches for distinct Factors
against their sequential typed attachment. Overlapping strong/strong,
strong/weak, and weak/weak same-Factor target cases prove exact Program-ordered
sequential Points, while alternative producers prove order-independent Point
confluence. They never obtain semantics from scheduler order or a batch join.
A declared atomic structural-row fixture proves that every
distinct-Factor Rule patch reads the same committed predecessor and presealed
row operands, that no Rule reads a sibling patch, and that a missing, extra, or
wrong-predecessor patch rejects the whole transaction before attachment.
Product-law fixtures use unrelated Factors and keys to prove
that a fixed exact read projection is
unchanged when those planes are added; no-read, Carry-only, staged-selector,
staged write-target, and explicit whole-plane-read cases have their distinct
callback-row, target-coverage, reverse-demand, and partition-count
expectations.

The complete semantic gate contains these counted families:

Before any production-domain numerator is admitted, the composition gate
counts all 16 carrier declarations and the complete Rule/reduction schemas,
independently derives every typed read-to-write/carry edge plus declared
support-prune and activation-family follower from those schemas, and rejects
any schema/coverage mismatch. The exact Rule inventory and operation coverage
catch a missing Rule; neither runtime logs nor a separately authored incidence
list can make the gate green. The resulting report must derive exactly
`K0/module/lifecycle`, the Effect singleton, `K2`, and Residence with the
dependencies stated in the reviewed incidence report. It separately proves that
the registered structural support-completion projection owns no Factor carrier
or incidence edge, that semantic Rules cannot read it, and that contradiction
Rules prune their typed successor support rather than writing a reachability
fact; incoming Program/boundary structure and those declared prunes are its
complete demand/evidence closure.
A future Factor causes the gate to derive and report a changed
condensation in canonical report order from its Rule schemas instead of being
assigned a dependency leaf or supplying executable edges.

1. **Program/Target/Link.** The generated grammar/use-context matrix covers
   every parser-reachable source form and every frozen Target operation. Each
   row has exact evaluation order, Value/Values operands, control/outcome
   successors, Guard provenance, boundary correspondence, and source identity.
   Metamethod applications, table delegation, continuations, modules, runtime
   TypeValue seeds/selectors, and provider lifecycle rows are included. The
   TypeValue seed cases separately cover primitive-before-direct-declaration
   priority, exclusion of nested declarations, every disabling local,
   parameter, upvalue, and pending-local-function binding, and the ordinary
   path for unmarked, aliased, and dynamic bases. A marked case has its Program
   TypeValue row and no mutable base read; an unmarked case has no such row and
   retains its ordinary lookup/call.
   Every runtime TypeValue equality/order row has exactly one Value/identity
   Boolean-result Rule consumer with both TypeValue descriptor reads and, for
   order, its exact Static-type subtype read. Exact proof yields one Boolean
   alternative, uncertainty retains both, and Numeric/equality supplies no
   parallel comparison conclusion; table-key equality remains object identity.
   Source-built and decoded shards seal to the same denotation and bytes;
   malformed or mismatched artifacts publish nothing. There is no AST, CFG,
   WIR, old analyzer, or decoder fallback. The report counts every admitted operation,
   its structural Target row, complete affected-Factor consumer set, unique
   writing owner for each conclusion, every outcome/causal edge (including
   Target-internal direct-call/access edges and their frozen yield disposition),
   fresh result, host exposure/member access selector/endpoint/`EndpointTarget`/
   direct-call selector-target-application reference/instance/correlated-result arm,
   exhaustive protocol case,
   initial binding classification, every `GlobalBinding` Cell/root/slot and
   actor-boot/rebinding case, Target `BootShape` ABI row, and every explicit
   exclusion. Actor-boot and Link tests prove that every mutable Heap, global,
   library, and Module-cache root is actor-local; roots within one actor may
   share their sealed representative, while an alias across actors is rejected.
   Send/Receive tests admit cross-actor heap transfer only with its sealed
   immutability proof and prove no receiver image, peer-state, schedule, or
   interference relation. Target tests thread concrete PRNG state through the
   exact `math.random`/`math.randomseed` relation; the abstract Rule test then
   existentially hides it, records precision loss, and rejects seed/history/
   result-correlation evidence while preserving `math.randomseed` Effect order.
   A source
   fixture using an excluded operation
   passes only by producing the exact unsupported-target diagnostic; it cannot
   disappear from the denominator or inherit historical facts.
   The matrix and artifact gate separately prove the static-containment closure
   and disposition for every static operand: contained terms have zero
   executable control/outcome/Call/import/Module/Heap/Effect/freshness/
   callback/provider transition, while an exact literal
   `typeof(require(...))` has only its static Link namespace resolution or its
   exact static diagnostic and its exact `ContainedOperand` known/unknown/
   invalid result, never a runtime Value/type coordinate. They also prove every source TypePublication's
   assignment-pair/path/frontier evidence, module export/return-root selection,
   `R`/`N.contentID` contribution, and source-built/decoded equality; runtime
   mutation/cache state cannot alter that namespace. Assignment rows prove
   left-to-right address evaluation, one RHS Pack adjustment, LHS-to-adjusted-
   slot pairing, and right-to-left Write commits with all outcome successors
   preserved through encode/decode. Every placement-facing matrix output names
   its one existing constituent-Factor writer and no `Placement` Factor/Rule
   conclusion. Module and resume rows prove the exact two-owner `ModuleInit`
   transaction: both single-output Rules read the same predecessor Cold state
   and presealed fresh-generation/materialization operands, produce distinct-
   Factor patches, and validate/commit them atomically without reading a
   sibling uncommitted patch. Later Pending transitions supply the
   Suspension-to-Module reads. The rows also prove both Module/Suspension
   incidence directions, generation
   materialization/retry/stale-publication exclusion, and the full two-input
   resume owner/read/outcome/invalidation topology without an Alias Factor.
   A source-built and decoded `typeof` occurrence preserves the same exact
   operand/source identity, containment disposition, and static namespace and,
   after identical Link/domain installation, derives the same canonical
   `ContainedOperand` judgment and the same exact static result/diagnostic or,
   only for `RuntimeSubject`, the same canonical symbolic handle. Neither
   artifact contains an interpreted Rule result.
   A source-built and decoded runtime TypeValue seed likewise preserves the
   same call-base occurrence and resolved primitive-or-direct-declaration
   authority; decode validates that row and cannot rerun binder resolution.

2. **Guarded engine.** Reduced Guard normalization, exact shared-support
  Product, sparse defaults, strong/weak update, read logging, reverse
  invalidation, dynamic relation activation, deterministic SCC/WTO formation,
  compiled-equation `Mu`, widening/narrowing, cancellation, immutable State,
  and demanded/full equivalence pass with synthetic Factors. A factor-free
  representation gate proves that Points alone are variables, schedule/WTO
  nodes, publishers, and Query roots; no Rule/commit/publish action or staged
  Point exists. Two or more Groups produce the same Point under adversarial
  declaration order; replacing or retracting one candidate recomposes the
  exact remaining join. A no-implicit-carry law proves that a Group contributes
  bottom for every undeclared plane and that only an explicit Carry transports
  a plane. Whole-Group atomicity proves every member reads one versioned ordered
  input snapshot and any stale, failed, canceled, missing, extra, or
  sibling-dependent member publishes nothing. A typed-read support-prune law
  proves an output-free prune may depend on multiple exact Factor units and may
  commit atomically with distinct-Factor patches without creating a
  reachability Factor or incidence edge. The factor-free cyclic support fixture
  and a nested contradiction/revocation fixture prove
   that support is a mandatory WTO coordinate, reaches its exact post-fixpoint,
   and added/removed regions wake no-read and Carry-only Rules without a
   Factor-key delta. Dynamic write-target fixtures prove exact-unit demanded/full
   equivalence, target-summary invalidation, and strong-versus-weak update under
   a finite presealed target surface, with neither a whole-plane fallback nor a
   second evaluator. Activation fixtures
   prove that argument, heap, and domain values admit only an eligible relation
   tuple over sealed constituents through its declared typed trigger Rule;
   they never invent a constituent or portable identity. Program recursion,
   Rule cycles, dynamic call cycles, callback cycles, and resume cycles use no
   Go recursion. A ranked Rule self-cycle and a mutual cross-domain reduction
   cycle at an acyclic Program occurrence each receive a deterministic private
   compiled-equation `Mu` without a Program/Link recurrence witness; adding an
   unrelated source recurrence cannot change that head. Conversely, source and
   boundary `Mu` rows alter only their exact Guard/substitution/evidence
   transport and never select the Point head. Late activation that merges or
   changes an SCC produces the same completed projection as the same relation active initially, including
  a history-sensitive lawful widening case.
  A nested localized-widening fixture separates external head ingress from
  internal producers, treats a mixed-input Group as internal while retaining
  its outside interface, resets the complete nested component on interface
  change, and passes local plus whole post-fixpoint checks under adversarial
  arbitrary widening proposals. A narrowing fixture proves the full-RHS
  sandwich law and well-founded descent; no gate uses fuel or a pass count.
  Guard validation proves exact
   normalizer preservation `γ_G(N(R)) = γ_G(R)` and includes a period-two
   live-interface reindexing fixture: both canonical phases remain distinct
   without allocating fresh atoms, contexts, or cache identities, while the
   ordinary finite-support Guard-lattice join and Factor widening/ranks
   converge it. No gate may require `(N ∘ ω)^(k+1) = (N ∘ ω)^k` or impose an
   orbit cap.

3. **Projection and reuse.** A subject/decision dies only at its exact Program
   death boundary and only through an owning domain projection. Equal read
   projections share equation work; different alias/root-role shapes do not.
   A one-key change executes only the reverse slice. Cache eviction changes work
   only. Reusable results satisfy the `FrameAccessClosure` preservation theorem,
   local simulation, exact
   boundary substitution, and final post-fixpoint check.

4. **Value and static type.** Runtime alternatives preserve
   presence/kind/type/identity observations required jointly by consumers.
   Static evaluation covers closed, symbolic, invalid, subtype,
   conditional-distribution, `keyof`, indexed access, `typeof`, generic
   bounds, and productive recursion laws. Repeated evaluation of one `typeof`
   occurrence under identical static inputs produces one byte-identical
   `ContainedOperand` judgment and exact static result/diagnostic or, only for
   `RuntimeSubject`, one canonical symbolic handle without reading State.
   Changes to only that runtime subject's exact operand Value/type coordinate
   invalidate and recompute the static-type Rule result; `Known`, `Unknown`,
   and `Invalid` have no Factor read, while unrelated Value changes do neither.
   Conditionals, bound
   checks, annotations, asserting returns, and boundary class validation are
   tested to consume that result and never a context-free memoized
   interpretation. TypeValue calls, reflection, and runtime comparison obey the
   sealed Target relation. Comparison proves the exact Value/identity Boolean
   alternative when its TypeValue and required Static-type reads decide it,
   retains both alternatives otherwise, and refines only through the ordinary
   Value truthiness/Program-decision path. The unbounded concrete chain
   `G, G<G>, G<G<G>>, ...` stabilizes through the Rule's finite declared
   summary descriptor, not a Cartesian descriptor universe, depth cutoff,
   recursive fabrication, or general Factor Top. Exact static `unknown`, known names,
   resolvers, flowed method subjects, iterator cursors, and ordinary pointer
   identities remain distinguishable whenever their own coordinate is known.

5. **Packs.** Closed/open Packs, nil-fill, scalarization, final splice,
   fixed prefix/open-middle/fixed suffix, constrained tail ValueClasses,
   bind/take/drop, repeated shared tails, non-injective boundary
   substitution, every outcome, and recursive prefix/offset growth satisfy the
   sealed Pack grammar and rank law. A boundary whose declared class contains
   `typeof` seals before Solve with its canonical exact static
   result/unknown/diagnostic or, only for `RuntimeSubject`, its symbolic handle
   and predeclared finite LUB/rank entry; changing the runtime operand Rule
   result requires neither schema resealing nor a new class identity, and no
   converged type result appears in portable bytes. A dynamic select/unpack
   case retains every
   feasible error/non-local outcome when its Numeric/Heap premises are not
   exact.

6. **Numeric, equality, and effects.** Primitive/raw equality, NaN, integer
   eligibility, fixed-template difference bounds, threshold rounding, and
   recurrence pass their concretization laws. Effects preserve authored
   duplicate rows and formals, exact boundary reindexing, finite may-operation
   union, explicit `UnknownExternal`, and negative proofs only under complete
   closed Guard coverage.

7. **Heap and access.** Exact/dynamic keys, raw absent/present alternatives,
   nil deletion, metatable routes, index/newindex delegation and terminal
   Throw, shallow freeze/isfrozen aliasing, frozen mutation outcomes, partition
   coarsening, strong-update licences, and deep lenses are covered. Repeated
   creation at one site proves atomic old-Recent-to-Summary
   transport across every owning Factor before introducing the new Recent
   object. Two retained loop objects cannot be confused, and a Summary object
   never gains singleton privilege.

8. **Calls, callbacks, modules, and resume.** Actual/formal Pack aliasing,
   captures, all outcomes, tail calls, open/complete dispatch, module DAG
   initialization/cache/retry, the Module-to-Value Ready-hit rule, and exact
   `ModuleInit` two-owner successor transaction are covered. The matrix proves
   both owner Rules use only their common Cold predecessor plus structural
   presealed fresh-generation/materialization inputs, their distinct-Factor
   patches validate and commit atomically, and neither reads the sibling's
   uncommitted output. It proves Module Cold -> Suspension generation
   initiation and the later committed-live-generation Suspension -> Module
   Pending correlation, with neither Factor writing the other's conclusion;
   fresh retry materializes the prior generation and a stale or
   superseded generation cannot publish into the new cache cell. Callback
   retention/re-entry and provider transitions use those same exact rows.
   Resume combines retained continuation Cells/pending Values/Guard with the
   current resumer/provider Factors through the declared two-input topology:
   Suspension classifies/consumes the generation, Value/identity transports
   aliases, Heap pulls back contents, Escape derives boundary reachability,
   Ownership derives duties, and Residence derives location/retention. The
   gate rejects an Alias Factor or an intermediate continuation heap and proves
   every owner Rule's exact continuation/resumer reads, outcomes, and reverse
   invalidation. `ReentryOnce` is consumed atomically. The cache matrix proves
   that two roots in one actor with the same canonical representative and the
   same resolved module traverse one Module lifecycle and share its Ready hit;
   distinct representatives isolate their Cold/Pending/Ready lifecycles; and
   roots in different actors never share a mutable cache cell. It exercises
   inherited and explicitly selected representatives at root ingress and every
   exact call, spawn, callback/re-entry, resume, and module-entry transport.

9. **Resources, escape, ownership, typestate, and placement.** Two resources
   acquired at one recurrent origin remain distinct through Recent/Summary.
   Releasing callback `c1` cannot release `c2` or discharge the underlying
   resource; callback Throw/Yield/Cancel retains the contract-declared duty.
   Deep-library substitution preserves containment, escape, mutation order,
   residence, portability, lifetime, and proof blockers without recomputing the
   child body per caller. Mutate/send/mutate/send scenarios preserve the
   contents observed at each Program occurrence; cross-actor cases accept only
   sealed immutable transferred graphs and prove no peer mutable state,
   scheduler, or interference premise. These are semantic facts, not physical
   allocation, COW, refcount, or GC policy.

10. **Evidence, Query, and diagnostics.** A must certificate covers every
    feasible Guard leaf, names every Rule and Program/Link boundary premise,
    validates the final post-fixpoint, and proves
    `gamma(Y restricted to g)` is contained in the claimed property. Precision
    loss blocks exact publication, including PRNG seed/history/result
    correlation after the state-insensitive random abstraction. A certificate
    that reads mutable provider/resumer state additionally proves observation independence or its
    owning-domain invariant for the entire use lifetime; a revision-keyed
    State fact cannot substitute. Query is pure projection and cannot close a
    relation, search owners, classify suspension, or infer a diagnostic.
    Gate 10 accepts no diagnostic, certificate, or semantic numerator until
    the independently recomputed complete canonical denominator tuple,
    including fixture/expectation inventory, host/deployment/Link, actor-root,
    root-to-representative transport, and root-set digests, matches the ledger
    exactly. It then runs the complete checked-in fixture ledger only through
    the canonical new analyzer and reports its full denominator.

Performance gates report wall time, allocation count, and peak memory for
Program lower/seal, artifact round-trip, engine compile, solve, Query, dynamic
SCC growth, Guard/product stress, heap partitions, recursive effects, deep
boundary substitution, and warm cache reuse. Exceeding an envelope is a
performance defect, never permission to change semantics or publish partial
work.

The generic engine gate instantiates semantically equal synthetic compositions
at widths 16, 21, and 25 under adversarial Factor, Rule, Query, and declaration-
source registration permutations. Every permutation must produce the same
`CompositionID`, Point/Group/SCC/WTO order, disposable in-memory synthetic equation
identity and topology, Query layout, State projections, and completed result.
An unrelated change in a different shard, or in a dependency outside a
template's closure, must leave that unchanged-owning-shard body/component
template's full canonical identity and bytes unchanged, even though the
whole-Solver `CompositionID` changes. A change to the owning shard or its Term
namespace must miss. Admission under a `CompositionID` mismatch rederives the
exact local closure and applicability coverage, reusing only an exact match
under that same owning-shard/Term-namespace precondition. These are semantic
permutation laws, not assertions about package structure or a fixed
registration sequence. Seal rejects duplicate/unavailable
unanchored Rule `SemanticKey` declarations under every permutation, while one
uniquely declared schema may produce repeated anchored instances whose full
identities remain distinct and canonically ordered. The production inventory
remains exactly the 16 Factors declared above; 21 and 25 are generic scale
cases, not admitted domain inventories or hard engine limits.

The measured construction envelope performs Factor canonicalization,
Rule-instance collision validation, incidence derivation, and admission-index
construction once per seal in `O(F log F + R log R + I + M)` time, where `I`
is typed incidence and `M` is the sealed module/shard index; emitting or
admitting `N` cache sections cannot repeat that whole-composition scan `N`
times. Changed-unit dispatch is linear in changed units plus compatible
readers, with no per-delta subscriber-vector allocation/sort. Read-log
replacement is linear in old plus new units, not touched sources times the
whole projection. Immutable correlated carrier construction may retain a
separately reported `O(F)` vector copy/join/widen cost at these widths, but no
hot path may introduce `F x R`, `F x M`, or whole-body-cache invalidation work.

No semantic test asserts Go package composition, imports, filenames,
directories, registrations, authority ladders, adapters, or production
implementation shape.

## One-authority implementation law

The live graph has one Program, one Target/Link schema, one engine carrier, one
Factor implementation per semantic domain, one Rule path, and one Query path.

Prohibited:

- adapters, bridges, forwarding packages, compatibility aliases, or feature
  switches between semantic implementations;
- parallel old/new Program, lowerer, equation, summary, effect, key,
  diagnostic, or artifact authorities;
- restoring an obsolete analyzer path to make an intermediate gate green;
- string/reflection/`any` fact exchange, per-domain Program scans, reconstructed
  CFG/WIR, fixed summary payloads, or Query-side semantic inference;
- structural tests that freeze package composition, registration order,
  transitional mechanisms, or an implementation noun.

Reference trees remain inert and outside every import/test/gate path. Valid
domain mathematics and code are retained when they pass the new Factor laws and
depend directly on canonical Program/engine APIs; they are not rewritten merely
because they predate this plan. Engine-owned judgments, duplicate vocabularies,
and adapters are replaced in place or quarantined before their replacement is
used. Semantic scenario tests may be re-expressed against the owning domain;
old structural assertions are discarded.

Each wave has one frozen input contract and one exit denominator. A discovered
missing shared operand stops dependent lanes and changes the single owning
schema; no lane invents a local substitute. Partial compilation or temporarily
red semantic tests are acceptable inside that wave. A second live authority is
not.

## Current implementation migration ledger

This is the authoritative implementation checkpoint, not another semantic
inventory. The live non-test tree currently declares 15 of the 16 canonical
Factor owners and 31 of the intended 39 unanchored typed production Rule
schemas. Effect is the missing Factor owner. The exact missing Rule denominator
is five Effect Rules (`bodyCapability`, `selectedCallRow`,
`outcomeCapability`, `boundaryTransport`, and `opaqueBoundary`) plus three Call
Rules (`dispatch`, `outcome`, and `callbackResume`). Transfer, Residence, and
Footprint still have no Rule in the intended 39-schema closure. There are zero
production domain Queries, zero non-test final Composition roots, and zero
canonical Program-body template compilers. The 31 live schemas are local
implementation evidence only: their derived incidence is not the complete
planned closure, and none of Wave D's four work packages is accepted. A
2026-08-05 executable-law audit initially found that `0/37`
executed their actual `engine.Access` transfer through the canonical
`Assemble`/`Solve` route. The production Value source and unary-not Rules now
execute through that route with nonempty runtime-issued accepted
`RuleDerivation` evidence and semantic Query observations. The Pack authored
source does the same for its canonical complete Pack, and Heap bootstrap does
the same for one exact Target/Link-issued present root entry. Canonical Heap
allocation ingress, empty construction, and closed construction join those
four accepted Rules. The sole Heap `IndexGetRaw` Rule now executes an exact
direct-constructor field read through Value source/allocation, Pack authored,
Heap ingress/closed construction, RawGet transfer, derivation evidence, and a
semantic Query while preserving the carried receiver, so the honest numerator
is `11/31` live Rules (`11/39` of the intended closed denominator). The Value
allocation `Age+Fresh` Rule also executes through that
route with authenticated evidence and a semantic Query; an earlier ledger
revision described its accepted law but omitted it from the numerator. The
denominator counts both Value Select arms because they are distinct
Rules with different exact read surfaces, rather than counting their shared Go
file once. Value's existing fixed `StorageTransfer` Rule now also executes a
real `local n=1; local m=n; return m` chain through the exact Link-issued
Bind/Read transfers, runtime derivation evidence, and a final semantic Query.
This accepts the already-canonical Cell path; it adds no Cell Factor, binding
vocabulary, transfer authority, or production evaluator. Value's selected-left
`and/or` Rule now also executes an exact false-arm Program/Link branch through
runtime evidence and Query while preserving the carried Value. The distinct
selected-right Rule remains outside the numerator: its two independent Value
producers require the real template topology, and no test adapter may
manufacture that merge. Helper/reduction tests, plumbing assertions, and empty-
forgery rejection tests do not satisfy that gate. Closing the remaining
numerator uses only the existing solver in semantic law tests. It may not
introduce a test-only evaluator, count as a real-fixture numerator, or assert
Go package composition.

The generic engine substrate has typed Factor, Rule, Guard, support,
SCC/WTO, evidence, State, and Query law suites. Those synthetic and hand-built
laws prove the generic machinery only. They are not a production semantic
composition, body compiler, fixture numerator, diagnostic oracle, or evidence
that a dormant Factor has executable Rules.
The Wave-D D0 cut is accepted: owner-only Factor mutation, the all-Factors-first
phase fence, one-shot fail-closed Seal, semantic-key-derived canonical slots,
and Rule-derived incidence/SCC reporting pass their bounded semantic laws. It
adds no production composition or fixture numerator.

The reopened Wave-C staged exact-read row is accepted. The obsolete fixed
read-candidate authority and Heap selector residue are absent. Public engine,
composition, and sparse-demand laws prove row-scoped exact selection,
multi-stage dependencies, sparse route replacement, cancellation, demanded
and full equivalence, Mu/WTO recurrence, replay identity, and additive cold
storage; the bounded full engine and composition/demand packages pass, and the
obsolete execution-spine negative gate is clean. This acceptance changes no
production Rule numerator, composition root, body compiler, fixture numerator,
or diagnostic numerator.

The matching route-preserving exact-write row is also accepted. `RouteWrite`
is the sole generic dynamic exact-target mutation path and consumes one
authenticated staged selection as an atomic batch. Duplicate selected routes
to one coordinate join by the owning Factor; declared Carry preserves every
unselected coordinate, including through recursive Carry closure; evidence
replays every selected tag/value/target pair. Cold assembly expands each
Factor-owned target universe once per Group in `O(R)`, and a warmed solve
allocates zero bytes. Independent source review plus bounded focused and full
engine gates pass. This generic substrate changes none of the production,
composition, body-compiler, fixture, or diagnostic numerators.

The first real Heap `IndexGetRaw` production topology exposed one missing
generic prerequisite: ordinary Carry could not age aliases in every carried
root-bearing Factor. A result-coordinate-only Rule was rejected as unsound.
That prerequisite is now accepted as one transformed-carry path with exact
ChangeSet/evidence laws. The same flash cut removed allocation's executable
Exact source, the Link host-enumeration projection, Heap's host descriptor,
and the per-host Rule and tests. Value allocation now publishes one atomic
Value `Age+Fresh` patch; Heap empty/closed construction publishes one atomic
Heap `Age+Create` patch. Heap `Age` is owner-fenced, idempotent,
join-homomorphic, copy-on-write only on changed paths, and allocation-free
with preserved immutable identity on unchanged finite values. Focused
production assembly/evidence laws and the bounded full
`analysis/domain/heap/...` gate pass (peak process-tree RSS 1,044 MiB). This
cut initially left the production Rule numerator unchanged. The now-accepted
`IndexGetRaw` execution law raised the accepted set at that checkpoint to
`9/31` live Rules (`9/39` of the intended closed denominator): one real direct
`({field=1}).field` Program/Link chain executes Value source/allocation, Pack
authored, Heap ingress/closed construction, RawGet selection/transfer,
runtime-issued derivation evidence, and a semantic Query. The Query observes
both the exact stored literal and the still-live Recent receiver, proving the
ordinary Value carry. A local-binding variant remains outside this leaf because
its distinct Cell/binding transfer Rule is not yet installed; no RawGet test
fabricates that missing owner. Wave-D package, fixture, and diagnostic
numerators remain unchanged.

Canonical Program lowering and sealing cover all 1,176 checked-in Lua fixture
files. That denominator proves parse, bind, Program construction, and Seal
only. The honest fixture-wide Program-to-Link gate currently seals
`1176/1176`: the recurrence Read-owner defect, backward-goto empty Pack
projection defect, missing nested-binary-arm cases, and missing nested
order-fallback Meta Applications are closed. Nine fixtures now produce a
complete semantic result: the real-fixture numerator is `9/1176`. The bounded
Oracle census sends the same canonical `911` fixture projects and all `1,176`
files through public `analysis.Analyze`; its frozen receipt is `9` Complete,
`902` explicitly Unsupported, `0` Invalid, and `0` Incomplete, with status
digest `d69d25346443ef839bab8982027e7f48b1ae3dc5440b43b1352b02c02cf3f394`.
The historical diagnostic ledger remains `911/911` suites and `845/845`
expected diagnostics; the nine complete rows now come from the new analyzer.

The former manual `value_transfer_external_law_test.go` and
`static_typeof_external_law_test.go` Program-to-engine assemblies have been
retired. Their owner-local semantic laws remain; neither a skipped test nor a
bespoke Batch is retained as a false gate. Wave E's one canonical body-template
compiler remains the first and only Program-to-engine evaluation route.

Migration disposition is fixed:

- Control's two-point lattice, default, rank, sparse storage, and
  self-read/edge-carry topology are quarantined. Its entry-seed and
  predecessor intent become Solver-owned structural support completion, whose
  registered Query/evidence projection has no Factor value or Rule path.
- Value retains lawful reduced-product, normalization, hash/equality, and rank
  mathematics. Presence and runtime-kind material remain Value subrelations;
  identity and variant-origin material is retained only over sealed structural
  subjects. String/path/formal identity constructors are quarantined. The
  current type-witness authority is split into the owning Value and Static
  conclusions; the current escape axis is a duplicate of the Escape Factor;
  gradual/explicit-top origin and assertion material survives only where its
  owning Factor consumes exact Program claims. Old state-lane inventories,
  portable Factor-value codecs, and registry authority do not survive.
- Current type sequence/Pack normalization, sealed label tables,
  widening/ranks, and context-free static type algorithms are retained when
  rebound directly to the Static and Pack owners. The monolithic runtime
  type/Pack carrier, its provenance product, and the old type-to-Value helper as
  TypeValue authority are quarantined.
- Effect Row normalization and compatible operation-label algebra are retained.
  Legacy signature/manifest lookup, runtime summaries, and their codecs are not
  Effect authority. Constraint arithmetic and difference-closure mathematics
  may be hidden inside Numeric/equality or Footprint, but textual `PathKey`,
  dynamic variable admission, and generic solver-service authority are
  quarantined. `formal/*` and `path/*` supply no semantic key plane.
- Placement is only the Query/certificate projection specified above; its
  stack/heap policy vocabulary and State carrier are quarantined. Typestate
  retains only lawful finite-FSM normalization/validation; its string-keyed
  store, duplicate escape/locality judgments, publication codec, and
  single-holder model are replaced by the canonical Typestate owner.

This is a flash cut, not an adapter migration. Before a target owner is admitted,
its old Factor, axis authority, store, codec, registry route, and semantic Query
path are removed from the live composition or quarantined. An old and target
owner never run side by side, and no wrapper, alias, bridge, or translated
carrier may connect them.

The 2026-08-05 Promptmap sweep and two independent source reconciliations
closed the historical Wave-B list to the following seven live rows. Everything
else on the former list has a direct typed Program/Target/Link owner and focused
law evidence and remains authoritative.

The 2026-08-08 Program ownership audit binds the Source/Flow flash cut before
implementation:

- Source owns the authored preimage: every authored Term cardinality and span,
  the source name, Nil/Bool/Integer/Float/String owner and payload without a
  causal successor, direct Body source order, Bind Cell order, and Function
  formal order. `Outcome` is the sole derived Term family: Source Build requires
  its count to be zero, then Source Finalize installs Flow's exact Outcome count
  and inherits each Outcome span from its proven origin Body. This final
  extension is excluded from Source's authored digest. Flow stores no span or
  duplicate literal payload.
- Flow owns Program-local evaluation and control: Values members and derived
  entries, TableField order, literal causal successors, Body activation, and
  the remaining causal/control geometry. Body parents and the statement-root
  pool are Source order/containment derivatives and are not retained in Flow.
- Source owns one private typed source-position index and Body source/root
  ranges. Flow's `position.Seal` supplies one explicit typed
  containment/position batch
  after the authored Source and Flow rows are fixed. Source validates and
  consumes that batch, retains only the declared offsets, roots, cursors, and
  ranges, and discards the batch. It stores no second semantic row owner.
  Identity and span cardinality remain total over every final Term, but the
  position batch is deliberately sparse: it contains exactly the least fixed
  point of non-Outcome Terms whose canonical containment parent chain reaches
  a direct Body source occurrence. The Entry Body and global Cells are
  rootless; the chunk-vararg Cell reaches the rootless Entry; derived Outcomes
  inherit only their origin Body span and never acquire Source Position, Root,
  or Frontier. `position.Seal` proves this exact sparse denominator and
  Repeat's actual Loop-to-child selection. Source validates only its owned
  direct-occurrence coordinates, Body forest, ordinary/Repeat geometry, and
  complete direct-source coverage. No sentinel, arbitrary occurrence, generic
  identity-position class, or fabricated root may fill a rootless case.
- `Program.Activation(term)` is deleted. A consumer that needs activation
  explicitly composes Source's position with Flow's `BodyActivation`; no child
  retains a sibling pointer and no root forwarding method reconstructs the answer.
  Repeat-loop frontier adjustment is included in the discarded seal batch, so
  Source answers the final frontier directly and Static reads it from Source;
  Static imports no Flow package. Flow consumes Static's owner-defined
  Finalizer and commits only Static-owned `CommitInput` after joint validation.
- Lowering/root allocation assigns one final authored `Term`. Source receives
  `(Term, Span, authored provenance)` and Flow receives
  `(Term, operands/evaluation geometry)`. Neither child mints, wraps, aliases,
  or translates that identity, and Flow constructors do not accept spans.
  The sole exception is the derived `Outcome` family: Flow Finalize assigns its
  dense canonical ordinals exactly once from the proved control topology and
  hands Source the matching origin-Body batch before either child is published.
- Lowering freezes exact keys in one Source-first construction phase. Its
  private collector retains raw literal payload references, emits Source
  `NameKey`/`ListKey` rows and the complete `ExactAtoms` denominator, builds
  Source, and claims exactly one Source Finalizer. Only that live
  `Finalizer.Preimage().Keys().Find` capability may translate the collector's
  raw payload references into final `keyspace.Key` handles for Flow and Static
  Inputs; those owners are built only after translation, and the same Source
  Finalizer is then consumed by Flow Assemble. The collector never imports
  `program/internal/exactkey`, assigns provisional numeric Keys, retains an
  interning map, invents a second normalizer, or keeps a Source capability in a
  published component. Failures before Assemble abort every claimed owner;
  once Assemble begins it alone owns terminal cleanup.
- Global Cells are pre-censused by the binder in canonical authored order
  before the collector exists.  A binder-scoped opaque global identity selects
  one reserved dense Cell slot and carries its canonical first source
  occurrence; lowering never interns or selects a global by string.  The
  collector pre-seeds each censused name into Source's raw exact denominator,
  resolves the final Key only through the live Source Preimage, and stores no
  name-to-Term or Term-to-name map.  Local Cells follow the reserved global
  prefix.  Runtime-type-value-only symbols do not enter this census, while
  ordinary reads/writes, static-query values, qualified type roots, and
  publication roots do.  Binder identities are construction capabilities,
  never persisted Program identities or Cell ordinals.
- The collector has one terminal `Prepare` handoff, not independently
  repeatable Source/dependent-input exports.  `Prepare` privately builds and
  claims the sole Source Finalizer, uses only that live Preimage to translate
  and build Static, Module, and Flow, claims the two sibling Finalizers, and
  returns one opaque copy-safe `Prepared` capability.  `Prepared` exposes only
  one terminal `Assemble`: all copies share one private lifecycle state, the
  single winner moves and clears Source/Flow/Static/Module plus Entry before
  invoking Flow assembly, and losers cannot inspect, splice, abort, or reopen
  any child capability.  No `source.Input`, injectable `source.Preimage`, child
  Finalizer, Flow Draft, or Entry crosses this boundary.  Any pre-handoff
  failure aborts every already-claimed owner; success clears the collector's
  construction rows and makes every writer copy terminal, after which only
  Flow Assemble owns cleanup and publication.
- Every rejected collector mutation is terminal for that construction.  The
  collector records the first exact error, permits no retry or later mint, and
  `Prepare` clears the unpublished scratch and returns that error.  Therefore
  multi-row operations preflight what they can, but they need no rollback,
  transaction log, or recovery path merely to make a partially appended scratch
  buffer reusable.  Legitimate forward references are satisfied through the
  declared preallocation/fill protocols; a caller may not use rejection as a
  forward-declaration probe.  Owner `Build` and joint Assemble remain the sole
  semantic validation authorities for facts unavailable at the collector
  boundary.
- The lowering collector is a construction cursor, not a fifth semantic
  authority or a replacement monolith. Its root exposes only Source, Flow,
  Static, and Module construction capabilities plus the one Source-first
  materialization boundary. Each capability is split into the same typed
  owner verticals as its final component, with fewer than sixteen fields and
  methods per leaf. There is no generic family mint, root forwarding facade,
  duplicate Bind/Formal sidecar, or reconciliation pass. Module census slots
  stay zero-based at the lower boundary and translate exactly once to final
  one-based Import Terms. A literal import request contributes only its raw
  first-fixed String payload to Source's atom denominator; final Request/Key
  resolution remains exclusively in Flow Assemble.
- `program/keyspace` owns the existing stable Term-family codes, family and
  ordinal projections, index bound, and the sole checked Term constructor.
  Construction rejects the Invalid family. The former use of a nonzero
  Invalid-family Term as a global-Cell ordinal is deleted: Flow storage carries
  an explicit typed local-Body versus global-key/ordinal disposition instead.
  Core retains no tag constants, raw encoder, aliases, or forwarding helpers.
- Source and Flow each hash their versioned owned authored rows once. Private
  deterministic indexes are rebuilt and excluded. Program content vNext hashes
  the ordered Source and Flow digests once plus each not-yet-cut relation once;
  it never re-encodes child rows inline. Program artifact vNext persists each
  authored child section once, rejects prior bytes, rebuilds derivatives through
  the ordinary seal, and verifies the claimed Program identity. A changed
  Program identity invalidates dependent Link replay naturally; Link's codec is
  not bumped unless its own wire schema changes.

Finalization uses one explicit acyclic sibling edge instead of pretending Go
has friend-only package visibility. Source, Static, and Module remain
independent and each issues one owner-defined, one-shot `Finalizer` capability
over its claimed Draft. Flow imports those three owners, consumes their
capabilities in its sole Assemble operation, derives the complete cross-owner
geometry, and commits each owner's private derived projection through that
owner's capability. Root passes the capabilities to Flow and receives one
opaque `Assembly` transfer token only after all four commits succeed. The token
has no child accessor or query surface: its sole `Take` atomically consumes and
clears the complete Source/Flow/Static/Module quartet, returns all four typed
components together or none, and every copied token shares the same terminal
fence. Root publishes only the quartet from that one successful transfer and
computes no child judgment. No Flow receipt flows back upward, and no
callback/interface, neutral capability package, generic DTO, dual read, or
compatibility facade is permitted. A published child retains no capability,
construction batch, or sibling pointer.

Acceptance is evidentiary, not an architectural freeze. During Domain,
Factor, Rule, Solver, Query, certificate, placement, or JIT work, any need to
reconstruct syntax, mint identity, reinterpret ownership, duplicate control or
heap semantics, or retain a private compatibility projection is an upstream
defect signal. Work stops at that consumer and moves upward to the highest
single semantic owner that can lawfully provide the missing relation; that
owner and its cross-layer law are repaired before the consumer resumes. No
downstream Domain or Solver helper may compensate for the defect or establish
a parallel authority merely because an earlier Program or Link gate was green.

The physical Flow decomposition must make that single-authority rule
unavoidable. `program/flow/internal/authored` is inaccessible construction
storage for authored rows. The lowerer imports only top-level `program/flow`
and receives its write-only Draft construction views; it cannot import, query,
or commit authored storage. Terminal Assemble consumes that Draft exactly
once. Relation-complete packages under `program/flow/internal/` (`body`,
`binding`, `containment`, `control`, `outcome`, `recurrence`, `executable`,
`position`, `evaluation`, `sourcecontrol`, `directfunction`, `candidates`,
`directbinding`, `causal`, `continuation`, and `staticcheck`) implement pure
derived passes over typed private owner views; they publish no alternate Program or Flow
authority. Top-level `program/flow` owns the write-only Draft, the small final
Component, the ordered Assemble pipeline, and its typed query views. It is the
only importable Flow semantic package, its Component is the sole Flow value
publishable by Program, and it is the sole Flow value accepted by every
consumer. No public authored View/Component/Cold, fallback, shadow comparison,
merged enumeration, alias, forwarding compatibility facade, or simultaneous
old/new read is allowed even temporarily.

The reconciled implementation denominator is binding for this cut. Source also
owns authored `Key` rows, the exact-key atom quotient/interning/canonical
enumeration, and authored `ControlFault` provenance. The exact-key normalization
and comparison law moves into Source; `program/keyspace` retains only the
`Key`/literal payload identities and owns no row, builder, store, or query.
Source artifact bytes contain only authored identity, spans, literals, direct
order, keys, and faults; its position/root/frontier/parent index is rederived.

Flow owns the complete Program-local executable relations: Values; exact and
dynamic Lenses; Cell/global/Read/Vararg/Bind/Assign/Write storage; Tables and
TableFields; authored Unary/Binary/Select rows, whose candidate-source buckets
are derived exactly once from the closed operator enum during Flow Finalize and
are neither authored rows nor artifact data; Function/Capture and dynamic Call
rows; Return/Break/Label/Goto/Branch/Loop;
the dynamic portions of ValueClaim and TypeValue; and Body evaluation ports.
Static owns Function type contracts, Call type arguments, and the static target
sidecars of ValueClaim and TypeValue. Source owns Function formal and Bind Cell
order. Module owns the shard entry.

An implicit-global occurrence is one authored property of its Flow Read row;
Flow may derive a sparse query index but Source stores no duplicate list.
Normalized exact-key handles on Lenses and TableFields are deterministic
Flow-finalize joins against Source's sole key/atom authority. They are retained
for O(1) queries but are absent from authored Flow input, digest, and artifact;
nil and NaN preserve the exact static operand with a zero storable-key handle.

Flow construction is two-phase. The private authored store retains only authored relations
and admits an authored universe in which the derived Outcome count is zero.
Source, Static, and Module each expose their authored typed views only through
an unforgeable one-shot Finalizer capability. Flow Assemble claims all four
Drafts. Its fixed internal dependency order is Body topology; Cell roles and
capture hosts; combined runtime/static containment; transient control shape;
canonical Outcomes; external direct bindings while Source Preimage remains
live; Source position layout and Source commit; typed
assembly-local evaluation Entry/Finish ports; private source-control geometry
(coordinates, structural adjacency, reachability, dominance, and exact
Label/Loop resume selection); recurrence; pre-Outcome executable closure;
local direct-Function proof; operator candidates; evaluation-owned pending
operands; the final causal-successor authority;
continuation Cell/Guard projections; Module entry; and Static
frontier/publication checks. The broad `forest` and `lexical`
packages are forbidden because they recreate cross-owner grab bags: Body
topology and combined containment are distinct passes, and lexical results are
owned by Body, Binding, Continuation, or StaticCheck according to observation.
Combined containment is one total single-parent ownership forest over the
pre-Outcome Term denominator, not an encoding of every typed relation.  Its
only roots are the Entry Body and global Cells.  A Body's parent is solely its
lexical Body parent.  Exactly one construction witness establishes that parent:
an ordinary nested block occurs directly in its parent Body's Source order,
while a Function Body, Branch arm, or Loop Body is established by that one
typed Flow row.  These witness classes are mutually exclusive even when they
name the same parent Body.  The typed construct remains a reference and role
label rather than an alternate containment parent; the retained edge is always
`Body -> lexical parent Body`, never `Body -> Function/Branch/Loop`.  A typed
child Body therefore has no Source Position merely by existing; direct terms
inside it still self-anchor in that child's Source order.  Reusable
handles likewise remain references: in particular TypeOf's operand and an
Annotation's Values are not containment children.  When such an explicit
static-reference root has no ordinary structural consumer, the joint seal
proves its scope and authored owner denote the same Body and uses that Body as
the root's sole fallback parent; an ordinary parent, a different scope Body,
or any unclassified parent hole is rejected.  Static TypeOf and TypeFunction
nodes use their local Static parent when present and their declared scope only
as the node's fallback parent; Annotation is contained by its target.  No
domain judgment, control reference, binding reference, or resolved declaration
is admitted as a containment edge.
Assemble then commits Static frontier validations and the Module shard entry
through those owner capabilities. Every Commit is terminal;
on failure Flow aborts every still-uncommitted capability and publishes no
assembly. Successfully created components remain local until all commits pass,
so no partial authority is observable. Flow returns only the opaque one-shot
Assembly transfer token; its atomic `Take` is the sole transfer of the final
four children and exposes no individual child accessor before consumption.
Malformed, consumed, or copied-after-consumption tokens return no component.
Root computes no child judgment. Derived rows are queryable through Flow but
absent from Flow content identity and artifact bytes. Edge ContentID lookup is
a post-root cold derivative, not hot Flow storage. No partial Source or Flow
component may be installed into Program while the old root rows remain.

Module entry is one private top-level Flow-assembly projection, not an
`internal/module` or `moduleview` authority. It consumes the committed Source
View, authored Flow, Body, Executable, DirectFunction, and Entry Body; emits
only transient `module.Entry` input; and immediately commits it through the
Module Finalizer. It preserves fixed Return positions for executable
chunk-activation Returns, exposes only exact Read Cells and occurrence-proved
direct Functions, and derives table members iteratively under exact Source-key
last-write and dynamic-key-fence semantics. Entry scratch is discarded and
excluded from Module ContentID. DirectFunction must match the exact Source and
Flow identities; no Cell-wide alias chase, generic path IR, raw string path,
or second Module result is permitted.

Construction provenance follows the same owner DAG. Body and Binding are
sealed before combined containment and retain only the exact Source and Flow
ContentIDs. Combined Containment and StaticScopeProof consume all four authored
children and therefore retain the four scalar identities in canonical order
`Source, Flow, Static, Module`. Every named result downstream of either proof
inherits and exactly matches that quartet: Control and Outcome; Evaluation
Ports; SourceControl, Recurrence, and Executable; DirectFunction, Candidates,
and Pending; DirectBinding; Causal; Continuation; and StaticCheck. A consumer
verifies every input result against the same four scalars before reading it and
stamps those scalars on its own result. There is no composite build ID, generic
provenance record, two-ID compatibility overload, sibling pointer, or persisted
proof identity. Replay reconstructs these scalar fences from the four authored
child ContentIDs; derived Source Outcomes/positions, Module Entry, and Static
commit receipts do not change those identities.

The construction lifecycle distinguishes Source Preimage from the committed
Source View. DirectBinding consumes exact keys, literal payloads, Bind/formal
order, Module imports, and Static publications while the Source Preimage is
still live, so it seals after Outcome and before Position commits Source. Body,
Binding, Containment, Shape, Outcome, Ports, DirectBinding, and Position are
therefore the pre-commit lane. Ports consumes the pre-Outcome Source Identity;
Source Commit installs the derived Outcome family, so a post-commit Ports seal
is invalid. SourceControl, Recurrence, Executable, DirectFunction, Candidates,
Pending, Causal, Continuation, Module Entry, and StaticCheck consume the
committed Source View or owner-local results afterward. No pass receives both
Preimage and View, and no wrapper preserves an expired Preimage merely to keep
the later order compiling.

The 2026-08-08 seven-action architecture checkpoint fixed the representation
boundary without changing this semantic composition. Source retains positions
as sorted sparse per-family ordinal rows; dense position membership tables are
Seal-only scratch and are discarded. Body and combined-containment proofs are
pointer-owned immutable results. Body Seal derives Euler intervals iteratively,
retains no child graph, and answers lexical ancestry in O(1); Binding consumes
that proof directly and may not reconstruct a parent walk. These indexes and
timestamps are owner-private representation, not Terms, relations, Factors,
Rules, or artifact content. The construction-owner DAG remains distinct from
the later semantic dependency graph/WTO, and no cap, budget, raw-family facade,
or second authority is introduced by these optimizations.

The complete combined-containment forest is an assembly proof, not a second
published Body-parent authority. Position commits its exact Body parents and
roots into Source; the full Body/containment proofs remain live only through
StaticCheck and are then discarded. Final Flow retains Static classification
and non-Body containment rows needed by Flow consumers, but no Body-parent,
Body-root, nearest-loop, or Body-ancestry plane. Cross-Body clients compose the
Source-owned Body hierarchy with Flow's non-Body parent relation explicitly;
neither Program nor Flow forwards or caches the other owner's answer.

Static observations use a seal-local Position-or-seed proof: a genuine Source
Position wins, while a positionless marked Read, Vararg, or Function follows
its existing containment Parent chain to a TypeOf/Annotation scope seed. The
seal first collects every endpoint's exact `(Body, observation kind,
observation Term)` descriptor, rejects conflicting descriptors even when their
eventual points coincide, then resolves that functional dependency chain
iteratively before reanchoring Functions. Cycles and unassigned positionless
roots fail closed. The dense descriptor/seed/resolution planes are scratch for
this seal only; this hybrid preserves Bind, Repeat, Branch, Loop, and function
phase positions without changing Source Position or the containment proof and
without retaining a second graph or result.

`control.Shape` is the next transient structural proof, not a control-flow
graph. It validates every authored Return, Break, Label, Goto, Branch, and Loop
against the live Source order, Body forest, Binding roles, authored Cells, and
combined containment, irrespective of later static/executable membership. Its
only retained rows are dense `Label -> owner Body`, `Break -> nearest authored
Loop`, and `Goto -> target Label Body` projections. The lexical cursor and
scope-parent tree, terminal-label adjustment, Repeat tail rule, and iterative
ancestry intervals are one-seal scratch and are discarded. Body owns the one
closed statement-root family law; Shape may not duplicate it. Shape creates no
Outcome, successor, CFG edge, reachability, dominance, recurrence, Mu,
continuation, domain Fact, cap, budget, map, or artifact identity.

Outcome identity is independent of traversal and map order. Flow first forms
the complete distinct semantic-key set `(Body ordinal, OutcomeKind ordinal,
target family, target ordinal)`, where target is zero except for Break's Loop
and Goto's Label, then mints dense Outcome ordinals in that lexicographic
order. Every Body contributes Normal, Throw, Yield, and Cancel; Return, Break,
and Goto keys exist exactly on their proved propagation chains. This keeps all
exits for one Body contiguous, permits direct dense ranges, and requires no
discovery-order allocator or second Outcome identity.

`OutcomeKind` is the one Flow vocabulary, with stable ordinals `Normal=1`,
`Return=2`, `Throw=3`, `Break=4`, `Goto=5`, `Yield=6`, and `Cancel=7`.
Outcome owns the nonlocal propagation selected by those keys: Return,
Throw, Yield, and Cancel stop at the current activation boundary; Break ends
at its exact Loop; Goto ends at its exact Label, with a same-Body Goto selecting
the Label directly and minting no Goto Outcome. Ordinary Normal continuation,
Label resume, and loop head/tail transitions are absent from Outcome. Their
one structural selection belongs to the assembly-local `sourcecontrol` pass
described below; the later Edge pass only materializes that already selected
geometry. Outcome sealing is iterative,
deduplicates by semantic key rather than source occurrence, and performs no
reachability, recurrence, Mu, continuation, domain, cap, budget, or
discovery-order computation.

`program/flow/internal/sourcecontrol` owns the sole structural control
geometry used during Flow assembly. It creates one dense coordinate space over
Body source cursors plus the required hidden Loop decision coordinates, one
canonical forward/reverse adjacency relation, the least structural reachable
set, and dominance intervals. Shape remains a lexical/control validator and
creates no adjacency. Outcome remains the sole nonlocal-exit propagation
owner. Sourcecontrol selects ordinary structural destinations but creates no
Outcome, Mu, executable membership, direct call binding, continuation, domain
Fact, public Edge, artifact row, or Program identity.

Let `N` be those private coordinates and `A` their one structural adjacency.
Structural reachability is the exact least fixed point
`R = mu X. ({Entry} union post_A(X) union availableFunctionBodies(X))`.
A reachable Function closure makes its Body available as an independent
activation; it is not an ordinary fallthrough arc. Dominance is computed over
`A` with one transient virtual root connected to Entry and every reachable
Function-Body start, so an outer installation cannot dominate an arbitrary
capture in another activation. All construction and solving are iterative,
deterministic, cap-free, and budget-free.

The assembly-local Result retains exactly five private proofs: Body cursor and
hidden-Loop coordinates, unique forward/reverse CSR for `A`, the bitset for
`R`, dominance intervals, and exact structural witness rows. A witness is
`Arc{From, To, Source, Target, Decision, Truth}`. `From` and `To` are the
topology coordinates; `Source` and `Target` are existing canonical Terms;
`Decision` is zero or the exact Branch/Loop whose polarity `Truth` selects.
No Arc kind, Guard enum, owner, span, Outcome, Mu, executable bit, or Edge
identity exists. Break and Goto witnesses retain their exact selected Loop and
Label identities while `From` and `To` retain the selected structural
endpoint. Witness lookup is indexed only for the closed Arc-source families
Body, Bind, Assign, Call, Branch, Loop, Break, and Goto. Function availability
and all construction cursors are seal scratch and are discarded. Occurrence
lookup is a fail-closed projection of Source's sealed Root/Frontier relation;
only a term rooted at a Repeat Loop at that Loop body's tail maps to the hidden
decision. Static membership remains solely the containment proof's
classification and is not duplicated in sourcecontrol.

Recurrence, executable closure, direct binding, and final Edge consume that
exact sourcecontrol result. None may rebuild structural destinations from
Source order, Shape, Branch/Loop rows, Goto/Break relations, or Outcome paths.
Recurrence alone derives SCC/Mu; executable alone derives executable
membership over the complete pre-Outcome Term denominator; direct binding
alone applies strict dominance and binding laws; the final causal-successor
owner alone materializes local Edges, typed Call boundaries, and live Outcome
routes. Executable never admits an Outcome: final causal sealing is the sole
Outcome-liveness authority, and consumers observe live Outcomes through that
causal result rather than a second executable plane.

`program/flow/internal/evaluation` supplies one assembly-local typed port
proof, not another graph. It resolves `Entry(term)` and `Finish(term)` from the
closed authored runtime relations and validates evaluation order iteratively.
It retains no successor row, generic operation/template, callback registry,
AST, or artifact identity. Final causal sealing alone combines those ports
with typed authored formulas. Select is evaluation control: its two guarded
alternatives use the existing Select Term as Decision and never invent or cite
a sourcecontrol Arc. When a Call is the left operand, those two alternatives
belong to that Call's closed typed boundary disposition; they are never hidden
Call-origin local Edges and never a second Select projection. A Call used as a
Branch or Loop condition instead resumes unguarded at that existing
Branch/Loop decision Term, whose ordinary local alternatives retain the exact
Decision and polarity.

Sourcecontrol additionally retains one narrow assembly-local resume
projection for existing Label and Loop Terms. It selects the first executable
root after the Label/Loop or the owning Body tail and exposes no cursor path,
edge kind, Outcome, or causal endpoint. Break/Goto terminal routing consumes
this proof; no later pass may rescan Source order to rediscover a resume.

A sourcecontrol Arc is structural evidence, not a causal Edge and not a
one-to-one Edge allocator. Each Arc has one explicit typed disposition: it may
materialize one structural local route, one typed Call-boundary normal resume,
only liveness evidence, or no retained row when its source is proved
dead/static. Arc coordinates and anchors are checked against evaluation ports
and the resume projection, then discarded. Evaluation/body routes and shared
Outcome propagation do not invent Arc citations. A Break/Goto occurrence
route carries its exact Arc/Mu annotation; a deduplicated shared Outcome chain
does not inherit an arbitrary occurrence's Arc or reset range.

The final causal-successor authority has two disjoint typed planes emitted by
one seal: public local `Edge` rows, which never originate at Call, and typed
`CallBoundary` rows. Each boundary has exactly one closed normal disposition:
one unguarded direct resume; two fixed guarded Select-return arms; or one
terminal tail-Return Outcome. The Select form stores the existing Select Term
and right-entry Term once and emits the two opposite truth alternatives; it is
not a generic arm relation, template, or third plane. Throw, Yield, and Cancel
remain exact unguarded dispositions on every live Call. The union traversal
enumerates every boundary arm, including terminal Tail, and exposes the exact
Select Decision and polarity for the split form. It never retains or adapts a
`CallDecision` graph or owner-local Select projection.

The causal authority owns one private outgoing traversal over the two-plane
union for continuation-Guard propagation. That traversal is an index, not a
