third row store or a pre-compaction graph: every traversed transition exists
in exactly one of the two published owner planes. Ordinary/JIT consumers cannot
mistake a Call boundary for local fallthrough, while continuation cannot
rebuild hidden Call edges. A direct statement Call may cite its exact
sourcecontrol Arc for its direct boundary disposition; Select-left has no
additional Arc and follows the typed Select formula directly. The sourcecontrol Result is
private to one `flow.Assemble`, is never retained by the published Flow
Component, and is unreachable from artifacts, Link, Target, Engine, domains,
or public Flow queries. Static exclusion comes only from Program containment's
sealed Static membership, never from a Static analysis-domain judgment.

Already-evaluated pending operands remain one immutable evaluation-owned
projection. `evaluation` derives them with the same typed, iterative
evaluation-order walker that defines Select event order; no Continuation pass
may repeat that family switch. The retained form is one private structurally
shared prefix store plus dense typed subject roots for Call and every admitted
function-style application. Its public construction result exposes only exact
subject `Count/At` queries over existing Terms and scalar Source/Flow identity
matching. Private prefix nodes, ranks, roots, and slice handles never cross the
owner boundary.

Continuation separately owns retained lexical Cells and live Guard support.
It neither imports, stores, copies, transfers, nor forwards the evaluation
pending projection. The final Flow component holds the evaluation Ports,
evaluation Pending, and Continuation results once as sibling owner results;
consumers read the owner whose fact they need. This is one authority per fact,
not parallel continuation snapshots. Go cannot move evaluation-private slices
into a sibling without an exported transfer protocol, callback, interface,
shared storage package, retained owner pointer, or unsafe access; all such
bridges are forbidden. Therefore there is no one-shot pending transfer: the
seal-local builder dies and the single evaluation-owned result remains
immutable for the Flow component lifetime.

Pending construction uses persistent fixed-width Term-set path copying with
dense count/prefix storage only: no map, sort, recursion, cap, flattened
per-subject set, public node identity, retained control graph, or cross-package
storage pointer. It preserves exact left-to-right evaluation, method
callee/receiver uniqueness, assignment address-before-RHS-before-commit,
Values fixed-before-tail order, table allocation/key/value order, Select-left
Guard exclusion, and the subject's own-boundary operand exclusion. With `E`
evaluation events and `S` subject boundaries, construction and retained
storage are `O(E+S)` up to the fixed Term-word depth; `Count/At` allocate
nothing. Executable and the complete Candidates membership denominator gate
which subject roots survive, but neither can change evaluation order.

Continuation's exact subject denominator equals Pending's without importing
or querying Pending: executable Calls plus executable members of the ten typed
candidate buckets UnaryNumeric, Length, Arithmetic, Bitwise, Concat, Equality,
Order, IndexGet, IndexSet, and GenericLoop. These occupy only the dense Unary,
Binary, Read, Write, Call, and Loop family planes. A live subject owns one
valid Cell root and one valid Guard-support root even when either set is empty;
a dead, static, non-candidate, malformed, or foreign term is absent. The only
query surface is provenance-gated `CellCount/CellAt` and
`GuardCount/GuardAt`. No callback, visitor, iterator, subject enumeration,
public set/root handle, Pending facade, or function/call-specific alias exists.

Continuation Cells use one persistent lexical-scope chain. Entry contributes
the optional chunk vararg. A Function activation begins without an outer
lexical parent and contributes Source formal order, its vararg, and authored
inner capture Cells. A Bind contributes Source Bind Cell order only after its
direct root. A Loop contributes its Cells only at the child Body entry;
ordinary Bodies and Branch arms inherit. Subject placement uses Source's
sealed `Frontier`, so a Repeat condition observes the child tail after its
Bindings. Every group is cross-proved against Binding role/host. Globals never
enter a continuation Cell scope. `CellAt` enumerates inner-to-outer without a
flattened subject-by-Cell plane.

Continuation Guard support is the least fixed point over final causal union
successors only:

```text
G(v) = union over (e : u -> v) of
       ((G(u) \ Reset(e)) union Decision(e))
Decision(e) = { e.Decision } when nonzero, otherwise empty
```

Reset is consulted through final causal Edge queries and is empty on typed
CallBoundary arms. Removal precedes installation, so the current edge's own
Decision survives its reset. Both Select boundary arms add the same Select;
direct normal, tail, throw, yield, and cancel propagate existing support.
Continuation stores only existing unpolarized Select/Branch/Loop Terms in a
shared append-only Guard-prefix DAG. It retains no Truth, Mu, reset range, Edge,
adjacency, sourcecontrol result, BDD, reindexing, or engine Guard value.
Construction first flattens the causal `Successors` union into Seal-local
routes, then runs one bounded BFS per decision rank and appends each admitted
subject/decision support incidence `I` to the shared DAG. With `V` causal
vertices, `A` causal successor routes, `D` decisions, and `R` total reset
memberships validated on local edges, its honest bound is
`O(V+A+R+D*(V+A)+I*W)`, where `W = 32*33/2-1 = 527` is the fixed uint32
Guard-prefix Fenwick proof bound. This is a representation-derived
GuardAt/integrity fence, not a semantic convergence or solver-work budget.
Thus the per-decision traversal, rather than an unsupported linear
graph-plus-incidence claim, is explicit; `I` may be quadratic in adversarial
programs and no semantic cap or budget hides that cost. Routes, marks, queue,
and interning
scratch die at Seal; retained storage is `O(S+N)` for the dense subject roots
`S` and `N` unique Guard-DAG nodes (`N <= I+1`), not flattened subject
support.

`program/flow/internal/recurrence` computes iterative deterministic SCCs over
the reachable induced sourcecontrol graph. Function availability affects only
the already-sealed reachable set and never becomes an SCC edge. A component is
cyclic exactly when it has more than one coordinate or one retained self arc.
Its canonical head is the least existing dynamic Label or Loop Term in that
component; a cyclic component without such a Term is rejected. Coordinates,
component numbers, SCC scratch, and sourcecontrol itself never survive Flow
assembly and no Mu Term is minted.

Exact Guard reset is represented once by final Flow Edge storage. For each Mu
head, recurrence builds one sealed stream of the existing Branch, Loop, and
Select Terms in semantic event order and assigns each member its head-local
rank. Every recurrence Edge retains only its existing Mu head and one
half-open `[first,past)` range in that head's stream. Reset membership is
exactly `slot(decision).head == edge.head && first <= slot.rank < past`.
Membership and indexed enumeration allocate nothing and are O(1); an empty
range remains a real recurrence. The private range handle is storage, not a
Program identity, public noun, or engine capability.

The semantic stream is not raw Term, allocation, or source-span order. Its
construct phases are: while = condition, Loop, body; numeric/generic =
one-shot header, Loop, body; repeat = body, condition, Loop; branch =
condition, Branch, arms. Therefore while/repeat feedback includes its
re-evaluated condition, numeric/generic feedback starts at the Loop and
excludes one-shot headers, nested loops form nested ranges, and a backward
Goto uses the exact target-Label-through-source-Goto interval. Crossed and
irreducible Gotos may overlap but remain individual intervals. Filtering one
structural interval to one Mu-head stream preserves contiguity. Structural
evaluation order is supplied by the typed, unretained evaluation owner;
recurrence does not infer it from Term, allocation, or source-span order.
Construction uses iterative passes and dense count/prefix/fill storage only: no
map, sort, recursion, cap, budget, persistent set tree, BDD, or per-Edge copied
reset set. With `E` authored evaluation events, `N` reachable source-control
nodes, `A` sourcecontrol arcs, `D` decisions, and `F` recurrence transfers,
construction is `O(E+N+A+D+F)` and retained reset storage is `O(D+F)`, rather
than the potentially quadratic sum of reset memberships.
Artifacts omit this derived storage and deterministically rebuild it; any cold
Edge identity hashes semantic head/range membership, never an allocation
handle.

The Mu head and reset-range start are independent coordinates. A while Edge
names its existing Loop as head but starts its range at the condition cluster;
numeric/generic ranges start at the Loop rank; repeat ranges start at the
child-Body event. No rule assumes that the head is the first reset member.

| ID | Status | Required relation | Sole owner | Blocks |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| B-HOST-CALL | accepted | exact host selector/output + direct-call Application + `EndpointTarget` operation equality | Link | Call, Pack, Effect, TypeValue |
| B-GSUB-TABLE | accepted | `gsub` Table-replacement access, first-capture-or-whole-match key, access route, outcome, and result correlation | Target row and Link occurrence | Call, Heap/access, Pack |
| B-ASSERT-NORMAL | accepted | function assertion + selected direct boundary + formal/actual Pack correspondence + Normal-only outcome | Link | Static, Pack, Call, diagnostics |
| B-IMPORT-CYCLE | accepted | deterministic Link rejection over the resolved module-cache ingress graph, distinct from valid Program Mu recursion | Link | Module and body-template admission |
| B-DEPENDENCY-ID | accepted | typed resolved provider/schema/binding-world dependencies and operation equality in Link identity, rederived on artifact replay | Link and artifact boundary | imported summaries and admission |
| D-STATIC-FRONTIER | accepted | one Static-domain `ContainedOperand` classification and exact `SourceFrontier` observation of the existing Cell Value; Lens inputs receive an explicit non-runtime invalid disposition until their exact access premises are owned | Static domain, using canonical Program Static and Link identities | WP1 and canonical body compilation |
| D-ANNOTATION-INPUT | accepted | complete TypeOf/annotation-argument `StaticInput` disposition and frontier denominator | Static over existing Program annotation rows and Link resolution | WP1 annotations and diagnostics |

`D-STATIC-FRONTIER` must not make a static term executable, add it to
`ComputationStorageTransfer`, create a Cell-to-static-Read bridge, or introduce
a static State plane. Program already owns `SourceFrontier`; Link already owns
the static operand, namespace, and universal Value identities. The Static
domain classification must give every admitted contained family exactly one
`Known`, `Unknown`, `Invalid`, or frontier-bound `RuntimeSubject` disposition.
Cell and Lens cases cannot be conflated: a Lens observation uses its exact
key/access premises or receives the dialect's explicit non-runtime
unknown/invalid disposition.

Program Static does not own that four-way judgment and imports neither Link nor
Target. Before Flow Finalize it supplies only the exact total static-containment
membership and authored operand/source/frontier prerequisites needed to exclude
static terms from executable geometry. `N`, `rho`, `S`, and the resulting
`ContainedOperand` remain entirely in the installed Static domain after Link.

A missing row stops its dependent domain work and changes only the named
owner. It is never inferred by another domain, reconstructed by Query,
implemented as an engine helper, or bypassed by a manual equation.

The implementation-bound test truth is equally finite:

| Gate | Current authoritative numerator | What it proves |
|---|---:|---|
| parser/binder/Program/Seal fixture denominator | `1176/1176` | structural source coverage only |
| fixture-wide Program-to-Link denominator | `1176/1176` | honest structural Link sealing across the complete checked-in fixture source denominator |
| reopened prerequisite owner rows | `7/7` accepted | owner-local positive, negative, replay, permutation, identity, and foreign/fail-closed laws pass through the bounded runner |
| production analysis Rule composition | `12` declared; all exercised across admitted arms | Value/Pack sources, Heap ingress/allocation/closed/raw-get, Call dispatch, selected/opaque Effect, Host Value/Heap bootstrap, and Value storage transfer share one sealed Composition and runtime derivation-evidence path |
| Wave-D work packages | `0/4` | no complete production domain family is accepted |
| final production Composition/SCC seal | `1/1` for the admitted subset | root `analysis` is the production owner; domains declare into it and Engine remains domain-blind |
| canonical Program-body compiler | `1/1` total compiler, `3` admitted arms | every mounted Body is classified as direct-field, call-effect, or literal-return; any other body rejects the entire Link with no fallback |
| real fixtures through the new analyzer | `9/1176` Complete; `911/911` projects classified | every real fixture now enters only public `analysis.Analyze`; nine complete results are detached/valid, the remainder are explicitly Unsupported, none Invalid/Incomplete, and no unsupported case is counted as a semantic result |
| new-analyzer diagnostics | `0/845` expected diagnostics | the historical `911/911` suite and `845/845` diagnostic ledger is reference evidence only |

No manually assembled external engine law is an entry in this table. The
three admitted arms use real lowered Programs and the sole canonical body
compiler under the one production Composition; they are not yet a corpus-wide
semantic fixture numerator.

## Current execution sequence

### Wave A — formal closure

- Finish the concrete semantics, Factor abstraction/concretization, boundary
  substitutions, termination ranks, persistence identities, evidence laws, and
  exact consumer demand table for every admitted domain.
- Amend Program/Target/Link only on paper for a proven absent structural
  operand; do not redesign an already sufficient relation.
- Maintain an append-only formal-closure review ledger. Each record is bound
  to the canonical digest of this plan and the current live analyzer
  source/test/gate inventory, covers the entire formal and implementation
  ledger, Wave exits, semantic/oracle gates, and every live non-reference
  analyzer path, and records every defect by stable ID with its disposition and
  verified resolution evidence.
- Run independent adversarial reviews until two consecutive complete records
  for the same bound digests report zero unresolved defect IDs. A complete pass
  rechecks the full bound scope and every earlier ledger row; it is not enough
  to report no *new* defect. The two passes are independent only when their
  reviewers and review derivations are distinct and neither adopts the other's
  conclusion without reperforming the full review. A bound-digest change
  invalidates the pair and starts a new pair.

Exit: one binding analyzer design, one same-digest independent two-pass review
pair, and no unresolved formal ledger row. The design closure was completed;
the 2026-08-05 implementation-bound audit reopened only the seven concrete
owner rows above. It does not reopen the vocabulary, engine architecture, or
consumer scope.

### Wave B — complete Program/Target/Link

- The 2026-08-06 composition audit supersedes the earlier claim that Wave B
  was structurally closed after seven owner rows. Fixture-wide sealing proved
  coverage of the existing representation; it did not prove that the
  representation had one authority, a bounded owner surface, factorized
  cardinality, or correct ownership. The audit found a Link-local second
  executable projection, eager dependent products, domain-shaped Link planes,
  bridge identities, an implicit mutable Seal pipeline, and permanently hot
  cold indexes. Wave B is reopened until the small-owner/component contract
  above is satisfied.
- First reconcile and freeze the complete parser grammar/use-context matrix
  and canonical Program relation schema. Every parser-reachable form must bind,
  lower, and seal through that denominator; unsupported source is rejected by
  the frozen Target. A newly discovered grammar or Program relation invalidates
  the disposition ledger and every dependent unaccepted Wave-B cut, returning
  work to this first step. No ownership migration begins from an incomplete
  source schema.
- Generate one complete disposition ledger for every live Program and Link
  relation family. Each family has exactly one disposition: canonical Program
  component, canonical Link component, domain-owned interpretation, private
  measured index, post-root cold artifact/rebind derivative, or deletion. A relation may not
  be admitted by an unclassified/default row, and no implementation cut starts
  from a partial ledger.
  The Link ledger contract has exactly four strict, versioned, LF-delimited TSV
  inputs: `catalog.schema` declares owners plus every typed declaration, use,
  and ownership import edge; `indexes.schema` declares every retained index,
  cross-owner hot/cold/contextual reference, and identity with its complete
  sorted preimage; `surfaces.schema` assigns each field, effective method, and
  semantic package function to one named surface; `residue.schema` assigns
  each nonfinal fact exactly one `move`, `split`, or `delete` destination. The
  historical four inputs and generated snapshot were removed after the Link
  DAG cut because they named a deleted Link-local keyspace and obsolete owner
  rows. No partial replacement is evidence: the
  generator fails closed until these four inputs are re-authored against the
  live Project/Boundary/Module/Host/Static DAG. No glob, regex, wildcard,
  default, inferred owner, or multiply classified row is accepted. The
  scanner type-checks the complete live production `program/link/**` family
  and its typed inbound uses; filesystem import text and reference-history
  trees are not evidence.
  Its design digest is one fixed length-prefixed stream over canonical
  root-relative scan facts and compiled inputs, the four raw manifests, the
  resolved Go toolchain/build/workspace/module context, and generator sources;
  it contains no absolute path, timestamp, map order, or generated-output hash
  cycle. Generation validates the exact fact partition, owner import and
  identity DAGs, contextual-reference issuer proofs, and each declared
  surface's sixteen-field/sixteen-method limit before rendering fixed typed
  arrays. Freshness is an in-memory byte comparison with `generated.go`, not a
  trusted digest header. Inventory mode reports sorted nonfinal residue but
  still rejects malformed/incomplete/stale input; final mode additionally
  requires no planned owner, nonfinal row, or residue.

  This exact partition includes structural representation facts, not only Go
  declarations and public surfaces. `surfaces.schema` therefore gives every
  retained field, array, slice, map, channel, named/method/other reference, and
  cycle fact one typed final storage disposition and owner; an unretained fact
  instead has exactly one `residue.schema` move/split/delete row. A public
  surface assignment, a private canonical relation row, a retained derived
  index, a contextual reference, and identity input are distinct closed
  dispositions, but no non-public disposition is accepted merely from an
  authored label. Public-surface storage joins its exact surface assignment.
  Every other disposition requires a same-scan closed storage witness binding
  the FactID, owner, carrier declaration, admitted implementation pattern, and
  its exact index/reference/identity consumer when applicable. Until that
  witness vocabulary exists, non-public structural facts remain residue and
  block Final. Final mode rejects any structural fact that is neither placed
  nor residue and rejects any fact classified by both planes. This prevents an
  implementation pointer, slice, or map from hiding outside the ownership
  ledger and also makes a residue-free final manifest mathematically possible.

  Final index/reference/identity rows contain scanner FactIDs and closed
  profiles, never descriptive prose. A query names its exact declaration; its
  caller list is the complete sorted set of typed call-callee Use FactIDs; its
  source list names exactly placed structural/relation FactIDs. One atomic,
  versioned pattern ID binds the complete cardinality, cost, allocation,
  storage, rebind, and replay contract; those axes are never an independently
  authorable Cartesian product. The same-scan witness supplies that pattern ID
  and the row must match it exactly.
  Identity rows contain direct source FactIDs and parent identity row IDs; the
  gate rejects cycles and computes relation and row digests from that graph
  instead of accepting a claimed digest. The one production scan classifies
  each Use role and emits closed implementation witnesses for admitted query
  and identity patterns; an unknown body is not inferred. Formal law evidence
  is part of that same witness, while an optional benchmark receipt is a
  canonical digest of empirical evidence and is never presented as an
  asymptotic proof. An authored law label is not evidence. Until the corresponding
  implementation witness and evidence exist, inventory uses a distinct
  `index-plan`, `reference-plan`, or `identity-plan` row. Plan rows contribute
  only to nonfinal audit/residue, never to generated live tables, and are
  forbidden in final mode.
- Before the first implementation cut, freeze a design-binding digest over this
  plan, canonical relation schema, complete disposition/query/index ledger, and
  generated grammar/use-context denominator. Two independent full design
  reviews of that exact digest must report zero unresolved design defect IDs.
  This pre-implementation gate does not claim the existing implementation
  already satisfies Wave B; the separate exit review below proves the cut.
- Flash-move all Program-local evaluation geometry from Link into Program's
  sole Flow component. This includes local computation roots, scalar and Pack
  endpoints, transfers, function/capture geometry, operator arms, and local
  storage/control projections. Link retains only shard fencing and exact
  Program-to-Target/project boundary correspondences. Delete every Link-local
  identity, accessor, index, and consumer reconstruction of the moved plane in
  the same cut.
- Replace materialized non-direct Call x Operation x Subedge and Application x
  Operation x ABI-port tables with exact factorized coordinates and sealed
  membership laws. Remove their stored product rows, empty roots, ContentIDs,
  reverse maps, and query-time binary searches. Coverage still has one cold
  total enumerator over the mathematical denominator.
- Flash-remove Link-owned analysis interpretations: heterogeneous Origin and
  ValueSource unions, RuntimeTypeAccess, ResourceOrigin/ResourceHolder,
  solver SupportRoot/predecessor planes, and duplicate effect-routing
  templates. Each consuming domain reads the exact canonical typed source rows
  and owns its classification. Target remains unchanged.
- Rebuild the retained Program and Link state as the declarative immutable
  child components above. Every component and semantic view satisfies the
  sixteen-field/sixteen-method limit, Seal is an explicit component DAG,
  final storage uses count/fill rather than retaining builder and flattened
  copies, and the root becomes observable only after all components validate.
- Delete the entire Link Candidate family. Program Flow is the sole direct
  function/body correspondence; Target-operation eligibility remains a virtual
  predicate over canonical constituents. Remove every Candidate row, handle,
  ordinal, ID, range, reverse map, cold reference, assertion embedding, and
  Application-operation/site/subedge materialization rather than renaming one
  into another. Collapse the remaining duplicate coordinates and replay paths,
  including ModuleInitTransition versus the existing generation
  and cache coordinate, ResolverRef versus StaticNamespace, owner-keyed versus
  shard-keyed static resolution, and duplicated Target effect routing. Remove
  the Program-edge scan that treats ordinary reads or writes of the global name
  `require` as module policy; sealed Program Import occurrences are the sole
  module-load source authority.
- Move artifact/rebind-only maps into post-root cold derivatives keyed by the
  minimal component digest they observe, and retain in hot components only
  direct indexes justified by a measured query. No
  operation-specific default, coercion, mutation, dependent result formula,
  Factor conclusion, or solver scheduling policy may enter Program or Link.
- Remove Link project recurrence/SCC/activation production, storage, identity,
  accessors, persistence, and tests in Wave B. Link boundary rows retain only
  exact references to existing Program source-control Mu and their typed Guard,
  substitution, and evidence transport. Link never computes or persists a
  project SCC, recurrence head/member, activation, or scheduler witness.
- Complete deterministic Program-core artifact encode/validate/decode, the
  outer envelope framing, absence/ignore handling for optional sections, and
  project linking for the same shard semantics. Wave B neither produces nor
  interprets a Solver equation section. Link accepts the canonical provider
  binding kind, call-independent exposure/member selector, exact direct-Function
  `EndpointTarget`, and direct-call selector/target/application-boundary
  reference; seals and validates every exact two-owner `ModuleInit`
  common-predecessor Cold, presealed fresh-generation/materialization, atomic
  Cold-to-generation/Pending successor correspondence, the finite module-cache
  universe, total actor-local root-to-instance mapping, actor-local alias
  equivalence, and the one
  deterministic
  representative per class; validates every exact boundary representative
  transport and every declared/resolved semantic dependency and operation
  equality; and derives its identity and artifact round-trip from those
  resolutions and the canonical complete module-cache ingress/transport
  relation digest. No solved State or Factor value enters the artifact.
- For `typeof`, preserve and round-trip only the exact authored static
  occurrence and operand/source identity plus the rederivable transitive
  static-containment disposition; Link preserves its exact namespace,
  static-only literal-require resolution, TypePublication export-surface

  Wave B neither derives that Static-type judgment, canonicalizes a
  `TypeofHandle`, interprets it, nor seals a domain-owned Pack class entry;

- Enforce a dependency audit proving Program, Target, Link, engine, and domain
  packages neither import nor translate the runtime-only legacy
  signature/manifest/`TypeInfo` artifact.
- Keep compiler, bytecode, VM/runtime, V2, and every future consumer untouched.

Exit: every admitted source in the generated matrix binds, lowers, seals, and
round-trips through one Program; every missing/extra row is counted; no
Solver/domain package is needed to pass. Program preserves `typeof` and other
static operands, static-containment disposition, and exact
TypePublication-derived namespace/substitution inputs, but their handles,
judgments, and domain class entries wait for the owning domain wave. Assignment
artifacts preserve the exact address-evaluation, adjusted-Pack pairing, and
reverse-commit transaction rather than a reconstructed source order. Link
round-trips and derives its identity from the sealed complete module-cache
ingress/transport relation: finite actor-local instance universe, every
analysis root-to-instance mapping, actor-local alias representatives, and every
call/spawn/callback-re-entry/resume/module-entry `rep(instance)` correspondence.
Its Wave-B laws prove that two roots in one actor sharing one representative
share the same cache coordinate, while isolated representatives do not and
different actors never share a mutable cache coordinate; they reject any
missing, foreign, cross-actor, noncanonical, or untransported instance row.

The component cut adds these mandatory Wave-B exit gates:

- the generated disposition ledger accounts one-to-one for every live
  Program/Link relation, handle, identity, accessor, index, codec, generator,
  and production consumer, with no unclassified/default or multiply owned row;
- a non-test static residue command reports zero live removed Link computation,
  domain-projection, project-recurrence, every Link Candidate family,
  CandidateID/candidate ordinal/range/cold reference, subedge-Application,
  Application-operation/site/port materialization, adapter, alias, and forwarding symbols outside
  reference history;
- the schema/codegen count command reports at most sixteen direct fields and
  at most sixteen methods for every semantic owner, component, view, row,
  builder, scratch aggregate, alias, constructor surface, and reference type;
- artifact decode rejects every Candidate form, virtual tuple, product row,
  domain interpretation, solver topology, and derived index payload, and
  canonical replay rederives every retained component and cold reference;
- identity validation proves the acyclic digest order `semantic child
  components -> root ContentID -> derived cold/index/cache data`, rejects any
  post-ID semantic mutation or hash cycle, and proves every persisted reference
  names only its minimal observed component/relation digest closure;
- the generated query/index ledger is complete, each retained index passes its
  declared allocation and asymptotic law, and scaling measurements prove zero
  retained `Application x Operation` and `Application x Operation x Port`
  coefficients while independently varying Applications, Operations, and ports.
  The measurement includes the transitive retained object graph, descriptor/
  class data, bitsets, maps, caches, artifacts, and cold derivatives—not only
  named product rows—while hot membership and constituent projection allocate
  zero. Static residue rejects every class-pair compatibility relation or
  opposite-side-keyed descriptor regardless of its symbol name;
- Program Flow imports neither Link nor Target; Link stores no Program-local
  Flow row copy or universal local/boundary root, scalar, Pack, transfer, or
  identity union; and engine/domain packages consume only their declared narrow
  capabilities;
- every semantic law, artifact/permutation law, complete grammar/fixture
  structural denominator, and bounded performance gate passes through
  `scripts/bounded_test.sh`; no Go test asserts package/file composition; and
- after the last relevant byte changes, two independent complete implementation
  reviews of one exact Wave-B binding digest report zero unresolved defect IDs.
  The binding digest covers the plan; schema/codegen inputs and generated
  outputs; every live non-reference Program/Target/Link/analyzer source file;
  generated artifact; test; bounded gate/residue/count command; module/dependency
  manifest; and the complete disposition/query/index ledger. Any constituent

### Wave C — generic engine and parametric boundary machinery

- Before the carrier cut or any Wave-C gate, remove or quarantine outside every
  live import, production path, generation path, test, and gate every current
  old-domain `Install -> Seal -> Solve` route and every old evaluator,
  body-compilation, and `EquationCache` route. This includes the current
  Control-reachability, Value, and monolithic type/Pack installers and their
  direct solve/cache tests. No wrapper, adapter, compatibility path, or
  translated cache may keep one of those routes reachable. Wave-C tests use
  only the replacement engine and synthetic Factors; an optional Program blob,
  if retained before Wave E, is opaque framing and not an engine cache route.
- Perform the equation/runtime cut as one destructive flash. Delete from every
  live path the Group-derived Point identity, recursive group-key machinery,
  Group-based committed inputs/queries, `RuleAction`, `CommitAction`, staged
  Rule Points, action graph/cone, action candidates, action-level demand logs,
  and the runtime group-epoch/commit protocol. Do not retain constructors,
  aliases, compatibility fields, build tags, wrappers, or structural tests for
  those nouns. The tree remains deliberately compile-red until the sole
  Point-equation compiler, producer/consumer indices, point-only WTO, Group
  candidate evaluator, Point fold/publication, demand closure, and executor are
  complete. Compile-green is only a construction checkpoint and contributes no
  semantic numerator.
- Before any Wave-C semantic gate can report green, a live-tree negative scan
  must find no occurrence in any non-reference build, test, or generation
  source of old `GroupRef`, `GroupAt`, `RuleAction`,
  `CommitAction`, action-cone/compiler/runtime helper, old
  `Install -> Seal -> Solve`, evaluator, or `EquationCache` route. Reference
  trees are excluded and inert. Tests that assert action counts, Group-derived
  Points, schedule event spelling, package composition, registries, or other
  transitional structure are discarded or rewritten as observable semantic
  laws; they cannot be retained to make the cut green.
- Begin with one carrier flash replacement/API cut. Replace the current
  all-plane Product, whole-fiber Restrict, post-hoc delta scan, erased
  heterogeneous dispatch, and repeated plane attachment route in place; no
  tuning, wrapper, adapter, compatibility path, or alternate evaluator may
  retain it. The replacement has one shared outer support and one flat sealed
  vector of typed plane-root handles, and exposes these exact generic
  operations:
  - a compiled typed `ReadProjection` from the sealed Rule read schema and
    staged selector DAG; `Product(ReadProjection)` invokes only its selected
    exact-unit or declared summary-unit partitioners, while `Carry` and an
    empty projection add no partition. The same compiled Rule schema exposes a
    sealed `WriteTargetProjection` whose nodes consume only those completed
    projected observations and yield only presealed exact/summary target
    capabilities; it has no whole-plane fallback or independent evaluation
    path;
  - support-only `Restrict` row views, admitted only after the carrier laws in
    this plan prove every read, write, Join, Widen, Narrow, `Mu`, delta, and
    publication path masks by the one outer support;
  - one carrier-owned three-region split passed to typed per-plane
    `Merge3Under` for Join/Widen/Narrow/`Mu`; roots may reuse only after the
    complete-region proof and no alternate root-combination path exists;
  - direct per-plane `EqualUnder` and `LessOrEqUnder` checks after canonical
    outer-support equality/inclusion checks, so whole-carrier equality and
    order never invoke Product or build row representatives/restricted roots;
  - an operation-produced exact `ChangeSet` containing support-added and
    support-removed regions plus changed plane/unit and Guard region, consumed
    directly by reverse invalidation—including no-read/Carry-only wakeups—
    without a post-publication scan of every plane or populated key; and
  - one structural acceptance transaction that receives validated
    single-Factor typed plane patches from separately identified Factor-output
    Rule instances only when their named Factors are distinct, plus any
    declared structural-output prune/activation members. Each patch is its
    owning Factor's typed update from the common
    predecessor under its exact resolved target capability and output Guard,
    not a full post-state plane. One Group has at most one writer per Factor;
    sequential same-Factor updates are separate Program-ordered Points and
    alternative same-Factor updates are separate producer Groups joined at one
    Point. The transaction copies
    the outer vector at most once and performs no temporary plane
    attachment/extraction or per-patch vector copy. No Factor-output Rule
    writes or stages outside its named Factor; a structural-output Rule has no
    plane patch. The transaction does not merge Rule identity, reads, evidence,
    activation, or scheduling. For a structural row
    that declares an atomic multi-owner successor, it validates the complete
    distinct-Factor patch set against that same predecessor and the row's
    presealed operands; no patch may read a sibling's uncommitted result.
  The carrier microbenchmark matrix and exact-key independence assertions in
  the performance gate below are mandatory Wave-C exit evidence, including
  zero-read, Carry-only, staged-selector, staged write-target, and explicit
  whole-plane-read cases.
- After that cut, verify the canonical Guard, joint sparse Factor fibers,
  typed Rule access, exact read/target logging, reverse-demand and dependency
  invalidation, strong/weak updates, the single point-only equation SCC/WTO
  route, compiled `Mu`, widening/narrowing,
  dynamic relation activation, cancellation, immutable State publication, and
  typed Query capabilities. The activation laws prove that trigger reads may
  admit only eligible relation tuples over sealed constituents and cannot invent
  constituents or portable identity; repair only the owner of a failed law.
- Make canonical Point identity the sole deterministic compiled-equation-head
  selector. Rule/Group identity orders only private transfer work. Remove,
  rather than adapt, bypass, wrap, or
  retain as an alternate scheduler input, any engine recurrence-witness
  dependency. Wave B has already removed Link project-witness production;
  Wave C proves no engine scheduler consumer or replacement witness exists.
  Program `Mu` remains attached only to its exact source-control relation, and
  Link boundaries may reference only its exact Guard/substitution/evidence
  transport.
  Prove this separation with pure Rule/reduction cycles and source recurrence
  controls.
- Require available unique Factor semantic identities before seal, canonicalize
  the complete Factor vector before assigning transaction-local dense slots,
  and canonicalize full Point, Group, Rule-instance, and Query-projection
  identities before topology or State layout. Freeze one admission index per successful seal and
  use sort-and-adjacent Rule collision validation. Declaration and package
  registration order must affect none of these results. State and result caches
  remain process-resident and foreign-Solver State remains inadmissible.
- Use only synthetic Factors: a shared-tail Pack relation, a partitioned heap
  root, a two-input resume relation, a history-sensitive widening relation,
  and a parametric synthetic body that exercises non-injective boundary
  substitution, local-binder freshness, read-projected reuse, reverse-slice
  invalidation, `FrameAccessClosure` checking, and exact outcome
  correspondence. The generic fixture also registers one exact structural
  support-completion projection and a contradiction Rule with its declared
  support-prune capability; that fixture owns no Factor carrier, Factor
  read/write edge, or semantic Rule read.
- Instantiate the generic laws at 16, 21, and 25 synthetic Factors under
  adversarial Factor, Rule, Query, and registration permutations, including
  history-sensitive cycles, unchanged-template reuse, admission-index scaling,
  compatible-reader dispatch, and old/new read-log replacement costs.
- Implement the generic equation/boundary machinery against those synthetic
  Factors only. It may compile disposable in-memory synthetic equations and
  validate their canonical identity/topology under the generic permutations,
  but it may not emit bytes for or admit an equation section, close a
  Program-body Rule-instance template, construct a production certificate, or
  evaluate an installed domain against a real Program body before the domain
  composition exists. There is no direct, ephemeral, or interpreter-style
  production Rule evaluator: Wave E's one body equation compiler and Solver
  path is the first production evaluation route.
- Prove that no engine package imports or switches on a Lua/domain package and
  that no semantic cycle executes through Go recursion.

Exit: the replacement carrier and all generic engine and parametric-boundary
laws pass with synthetic Factors, including the required carrier benchmark and
exact-key-independence matrix. The engine can execute arbitrary declared
Factors without knowing their meaning, but no domain-dependent persistence
closure has occurred.

### Wave D — declare domains and close the derived dependency graph

Wave D begins by completing D0 before implementing or installing another
production domain. Each semantic owner then declares only its typed Factor
carrier; its children separately declare Rules, reductions, evidence checkers,
and single-owner Queries from explicit typed owner capabilities. Each
multi-Factor diagnostic/certificate output family, including the placement
certificate, declares one dedicated consumer/query child with only its named
typed read capabilities and completed State/evidence; it is not a domain,
Rule path, or composition-root helper. Composition lists only those
declarations, never executable incidence edges. It first declares all
16 typed Factor carriers, then binds the complete production Rule/reduction set
and every staged read and write-target DAG in one candidate domain composition,
derives and validates the typed read-to-write/carry graph, reverse target
surfaces, and declared support-prune and activation-family followers from those
sealed schemas, computes its SCC
condensation and canonical report encoding, and seals once, closing only whole
derived components. That cold validation creates no Factor evaluator,
worklist, dirty set, widening loop, runtime schedule, or phase. No numbered
inventory entry, declaration call order, or implementation work package is a
semantic install order.

The following numbered groups are implementation work packages only; they may
produce code and law suites independently but confer no semantic closure:

1. The registered structural support-completion projection, Value/identity,
   Static type, TypeValue, Pack, Numeric/equality, and Effect. The context-free static evaluator
   derives each `ContainedOperand` judgment from the existing `StaticInput`;
   only its `RuntimeSubject` case has a State-dependent Rule, which declares
   that exact operand Value/type coordinate read. Pack boundary schemas use
   the corresponding exact static result/unknown or runtime subject and its
   predeclared finite class-LUB/rank entry without requesting a Rule result,
   and no construction-time dependency reads State.
2. Heap/key partitions, raw presence, metatable access/delegation,
   containment/role aging, Call endpoint alternatives, and open/complete
   dispatch.
3. Module boundaries/cache state, provider lifecycle correspondences,
   two-input Suspension, and Typestate resource holder counts/cleanup duty.
4. Escape, Ownership obligations, Transfer, Residence,
   mutation/lifetime/Footprint, placement-facing facts, and the smallest
   justified cross-domain reductions.

Only after all four work packages have supplied their declarations does the
composition derive the current condensation report: `K0/module/lifecycle` is
the predecessor of Effect and `K2`, and `K2` is the predecessor of Residence.
The registered structural support-completion projection remains outside the
semantic SCC graph. This report must exactly match the reviewed expected
incidence report above, which is a gate oracle rather than an executable input.
A missing operand
returns to Wave B's single schema owner; a missing invariant returns to its
unique conclusion-owning domain. Neither becomes an engine helper, generic
relation service, adapter, or reason to impose a false dependency leaf.

Exit: the final sealed domain composition is ready for production evaluation.
Every required Factor, Rule, reduction, evidence checker, and Query declaration
passes its local abstraction/concretization, lattice, default/presence,
monotonicity, boundary-substitution, widening and applicable cyclic-member
rank/termination, evidence, and
Program-supply laws. The derived inter-domain dependency graph is closed. The
composition gate
independently derives every typed read-to-write/carry edge and declared
support-prune/activation-family follower from the exact sealed Rule inventory,
rejects schema or coverage mismatch, and verifies the reviewed current SCC
condensation report, including the support-completion non-Factor law. Runtime
logs and separately authored incidence lists cannot satisfy this gate or hide
a missing Rule.
It first rejects duplicate/unavailable unanchored Rule `SemanticKey`
declarations, then derives the whole-Solver schema-level canonical
`CompositionID` from those results and the already sealed Factor, unanchored
Rule-schema, engine/compiler, and widening identities; no Program/Link-
anchored Rule instance, composition manifest, or persisted execution schedule
is admitted.
No real Program body has been evaluated, no production State has been
published, and no domain-dependent template, persistence, or certificate
closure has been admitted. A direct or ephemeral production evaluator is
forbidden; readiness does not count as complete-denominator convergence.

### Wave E — exact body templates, persistence, and certificates

- Wave E begins with one deliberately narrow but fully production vertical
  slice; it does not wait for every later Factor and Rule and it does not claim
  the complete-domain denominator. The first admitted body shape is the exact
  direct-field read used by `({field=1}).field`. Its canonical Program/Link
  lowering must issue all Points, occurrences, operands, and boundaries. Source
  may issue only its Body order, parent/root, position, and Entry facts; Flow
  may issue only its authored evaluation, executable, access, and control
  geometry. Neither owner may merge or reconstruct the other's plane. The
  composition root may create one transient `ProgramBodyBinding` after exact
  Program, Flow, Link, shard, and Body fencing. That binding contains only
  opaque owner-issued subcapabilities and those exact owner fences; it copies
  no rows, arrays, graph, or identity and is never retained or serialized.

  One opaque engine assembly capability may expose only owner-issued Point,
  Member, Group, boundary, and Query capabilities over the existing `engine.Assembly`;
  it exposes no `equation.Batch`, callback, raw equation value, declaration,
  semantic key constructor, SCC/WTO input, or alternate Solver. The existing
  activation-specific `SourceBuild` is not this capability and may not be
  reused as a second source/topology authority. Flow Causal edges are not
  translated wholesale into engine Inputs: this direct-field body derives its
  receiver, key, allocation, and access relations from exact authored,
  executable, AccessGeometry, and Heap Topology rows. A Causal Edge is consumed
  only by a Rule whose declared semantics actually observes that exact Edge.
  Link Boundary remains the owner of cross-Program/Target transport and may
  not persist an Application-by-Operation product or copy Flow geometry.

  That first body compiles exactly six distinct production Rule instances:
  Value source, Pack authored source, Heap allocation ingress, Value
  `Age+Fresh` allocation, Heap `Age+Create` closed construction, and Heap
  `RawGet`. P0 contains the three zero-input source members. P1 contains the
  Value and Heap one-input members sharing one ordered predecessor vector only
  because they write distinct Factors. P2 contains `RawGet` with exactly four
  ordered inputs `(Value, Call, Heap, Pack)`; its `sourceRead` is another read
  from the Value input, not a fifth input. The exact transports are Value
  P1-to-P2, Heap P1-to-P2, Pack P0-to-P2, and Call P0-to-P2; Call carries its
  empty/default value because this body contains no Call row. P1 does not
  manufacture Pack or Call carry. No Group contains two writers of one Factor.
  The composition root issues the complete versioned semantic-key bundle for
  Factors, Rules, evidence, Points, occurrences, boundaries, and the Query;
  domain packages consume those typed keys and may derive only owner-local
  descendants from an already-issued owner key. A body facade never mints a
  key or Point order. The composition root, not Value or Heap, owns the Query over the
  completed P2 State. Query materialization occurs only after `SolveComplete`
  and cannot feed another solve. Every Rule uses runtime-issued derivation
  evidence; no `analysis/engine/testlaw`, manual Batch, direct reducer, injected
  domain value, or Query-side inference is admissible. Unsupported body rows
  fail closed rather than being interpreted by a fallback.

  This slice is the first case of the sole canonical body compiler, not a
  temporary Oracle adapter. Its exit law seals a real Program and Link, derives
  the six exact instances from their owner rows, assembles once, calls
  `Solver.Solve` exactly once, and obtains the field result through the
  root-owned Query. It proves the production composition seam and the single
  semantic fixed-point boundary. It does not yet prove general Lua coverage,
  interprocedural summaries, actor placement, or the full Rule denominator.

- Extend that same canonical production equation compiler to compile one
  entry-independent
  equation template per Program body/component and rederive its complete
  Rule-instance closure from that final Factor/Rule composition. Each template
  identity is assessed within its unchanged owning shard and retained Term
  namespace and then by its exact engine equation-schema and complete local
  anchored Rule-instance, Factor-incidence, structural, boundary, and
  applicability closure; the whole-Solver `CompositionID` is provenance, never
  a template identity component. This is the first and only production route for
  evaluating installed Rules over real Program bodies; no direct, ephemeral,
  or interpreter-style evaluator exists. The canonical instance identity retains
  `At(shard,term)|From(shard,exact Edge)|Relation(exact Application)`, output,
  ordered reads/exact keys, and its exact-or-staged write-target disposition
  with the sealed target surface and coverage/closure-law identities. Repeated
  uses of one Rule schema at distinct anchors are ordinary distinct instances.
  The executable Point/Group hypergraph and the persisted closure are projections of
  these same rows, never independently reconstructed authorities.
- Apply the Wave C generic boundary machinery to those exact Rules: validate
  non-injective substitution, local-binder freshness, read-projected reuse,
  reverse-slice invalidation, `FrameAccessClosure` checking, and exact outcome
  correspondence.
- Add the Solver-owned optional equation-section codec/admission path and
  persist only equation topology whose complete canonical compiler image and
  Rule-instance closure have been rederived and exactly validated against the
  current Program/Link and installed Factor/Rule schema under dependency-minimal
  identities. Each body/component names only its exact engine equation-schema
  and, within its unchanged owning shard and retained Term namespace, local
  Factor-incidence, Rule-instance, structural, boundary, and applicability
  closure. A `CompositionID` mismatch triggers exact local rederivation and
  permits reuse only when that closure and applicability
  coverage match under the unchanged owning-shard/Term-namespace precondition;
  otherwise it is an ordinary dependency-minimal miss. An unrelated change in
  a different shard, or in a dependency outside that closure, therefore
  preserves an unchanged-owning-shard template's canonical identity and bytes;
  any owning-shard or Term-namespace change is a miss. A
  source-control `Mu` in that section is a verified core
  reference, never a copied recurrence authority. A solved specialization
  remains operational and must revalidate its input projection and final
  post-fixpoint.
- The optional equation-template byte-equality gate begins only here, after
  that canonical production compiler and codec exist. Equivalent production
  template compilations/admissions for the same unchanged owning shard and
  Term namespace plus one exact local closure must be byte-identical. An
  equivalent rebuilt owning shard is a miss, not a remapping/re-encoding
  requirement. Wave-C disposable synthetic in-memory identity/topology is not
  a byte or section substitute.
- Version the canonical Factor/Rule ordering in the optional equation schema
  and engine producer. A declaration-ordered section from an older version is
  discarded and rebuilt as an ordinary optional-section miss while its
  Program core remains admissible; there is no old ordering decoder or
  fallback. Reuse the seal-frozen admission index for every section so
  emitting/admitting many shards cannot rescan the whole composition per
  section.
- Keep the semantic instance of a selected dynamic relation equation-epoch
  local. Retain its validated eligible constituent tuple only across topology
  rebuilds inside the same solve session and input revision, as specified by
  the canonical lifecycle above; cancellation, publication, or input revision
  ends the session and discards that set and its admission evidence. Each fresh
  equation epoch rebuilds Point values, Group proposals, supports,
  reads/reverse buckets, dirty state, SCC/WTO instances, and evidence against
  that session-local accepted set. Each accepted relation re-enters the sole
  Point/Group equation compiler and generic SCC/WTO path; it neither mutates a
  portable body template nor creates a dynamic-relation scheduler or
  persistence section.
- Replace any whole-Link body/template cache key with the exact sealed
  boundary/provider/import dependency digest observed by that template. A
  whole-Link key is a sound conservative miss during the wave, but the
  unchanged-owning-shard dependency-minimal reuse exit cannot pass while it
  remains.
- Implement constructive evidence over the installed Rules, Guard cover,
  Program/Link boundaries, compiled-equation `Mu` backreferences, and the
  observation-independence/lifetime-invariant gate for every future-consumer
  certificate that reads mutable provider/resumer state.
- Implement the canonical typed future-consumer certificate codec and its
  independent checker. The codec contains only the eligible semantic theorem,
  polarity, exact Program/Link/Target identities, dominating Guard,
  typed-evidence root, checker/domain versions, and named precision blockers;
  it contains no State root, Factor value, Solver coordinate, schedule, read
  log, or consumer policy. Equivalent checked certificates are byte-identical,
  a foreign/stale/malformed certificate fails closed, and two independent
  consumers obtain the same theorem without re-solving or reconstructing a
  memory/type/placement judgment.
- Run complete-denominator Semantic Gates 4–9 through those canonical body
  templates and the one Solver. Every admitted Program root must converge with
  its owning domain laws and no Query-derived judgment; no alternate evaluator
  may supply a gate numerator.

Exit: every semantic family in Gates 4–9 converges over the complete Program
denominator through the canonical production templates and Solver. Equal formal
projections share work without merging caller State or freshness; changed
relevant inputs invalidate exactly their reverse slice; recursive body reuse,
persisted templates, and certificates contain no second summary authority or
production evaluation path.

### Wave F — Queries and the honest oracle

- Implement diagnostics, provider facts, and consumer certificates as pure
  projections of completed State. Each multi-Factor diagnostic/certificate
  family is its own declared consumer/query child with only its named typed
  read capabilities and completed evidence; in particular the placement
  certificate has no placement Factor, Rule, State, closure, or semantic
  output beyond that projection.
- Before any Wave-F numerator, independently rebuild and exactly match the
  complete canonical denominator tuple: fixture source/expectation inventory,
  Target, host/deployment/Link resolved-module relation, actor boots and
  actor-local roots, root-to-`ModuleCacheInstance` representative transport,
  Query/diagnostic schema, and ordered root-set digests. A mismatch is a red
  inventory failure, not an unsupported result or a reason to omit a root.
- Run the complete checked-in fixture expectation ledger only through the
  canonical analyzer after independently recomputing and matching every
  expectation-manifest schema/content digest. Add a real diagnostic or repair
  the owning Factor/Rule; never restore an old path or weaken the denominator.
- Run Program coverage, convergence, persistence, demanded/full equivalence,
  deterministic output, incomplete-root accounting, and performance gates
  together.

Exit: the declared source/root/diagnostic denominators are fully reported and
green; repeated completed runs are byte-identical; no old analyzer or false
gate contributes a result.

Future compiler/runtime/JIT integration is a separate authorization after Wave
F. This plan only ensures Program and certificates contain the semantics that a
future consumer needs.

## Parallel work discipline

Parallel lanes begin only after their shared input schema is frozen. One lane
owns each child domain or independent law family; one integrator owns
Program/Target/Link schema changes and one owns engine composition. Lanes may
not edit a shared contract independently, introduce a temporary vocabulary, or
land a row/helper without its complete laws and denominator. Every lane reports
which prior gate it consumed, which exact exit row it closed, and which files it
changed. Fanout accelerates independent leaves; it never substitutes motion for
a green invariant.
